<a id="mtp-000187"></a>
<!-- pair: MTP-000187; format: prose; role: main_text -->

དེ་ནས་རིགས་ཀྱི་ཁྲོ་བོ་རྣམས་ཀྱིས་འདི་སྐད་ཅེས་གསོལ་ཏོ

Then the wrathful ones of the families petitioned in these words:[^T05-001][^MTP-AUDIT-C05-001][^MTP-AUDIT-C05-003]

<a id="mtp-000188"></a>
<!-- pair: MTP-000188; format: verse; role: main_text -->

ཀྱེ་ཀྱེ་བཅོམ་ལྡན་རྡོ་རྗེ་འཆང  
སངས་རྒྱས་ཀུན་གྱི་རང་བཞིན་གང  
སེམས་ཅན་རྣམས་ལ་ཇི་ལྟར་གནས

“O, O Blessed One Vajradhara!  
What is the intrinsic nature of all buddhas?  
How does it abide in karmic beings?[^T05-001][^T05-002][^CH05-G000640][^MTP-AUDIT-C05-001][^MTP-AUDIT-C05-003][^MTP-AUDIT-C05-004][^MTP-AUDIT-C05-005][^MTP-AUDIT-C05-006]

<a id="mtp-000189"></a>
<!-- pair: MTP-000189; format: verse; role: main_text -->

དེ་ཉིད་གནས་ན་འཁྲུལ་མི་རིགས  
འཁྲུལ་དེ་ཇི་ལྟར་ལྡོག་ཚུལ་ནི  
གང་དང་གང་གིས་ལྡོག་པ་ལགས  
མ་འཁྲུལ་ཡེ་ཤེས་ཇི་ལྟ་བུ

If that itself abides, delusion would be unfitting.  
As for the manner in which that delusion is reversed,  
by what and by what is it reversed?  
What is undeluded primordial knowing like?[^T05-002][^MTP-AUDIT-C05-001][^MTP-AUDIT-C05-003][^MTP-AUDIT-C05-007][^MTP-AUDIT-C05-008][^MTP-AUDIT-C05-009][^MTP-AUDIT-C05-010]

<a id="mtp-000190"></a>
<!-- pair: MTP-000190; format: verse; role: main_text -->

བདག་ཅག་རྣམས་ནི་གྱུར་ན  
དེ་ལ་གཉིས་སྣང་ཇི་ལྟར་ལགས  
གཙོ་བོ་ཐེ་ཚོམ་ཇི་ལྟར་བསལ

If we have become [—],  
how is there twofold appearance in that?  
Lord, how is doubt dispelled?”[^T05-002][^CH05-G000645][^MTP-AUDIT-C05-001][^MTP-AUDIT-C05-003][^MTP-AUDIT-C05-011][^MTP-AUDIT-C05-012][^MTP-AUDIT-C05-013]

<a id="mtp-000191"></a>
<!-- pair: MTP-000191; format: prose; role: source_metadata -->

> [source_metadata] ༤༥༥

> [source_metadata] [Printed page 455.][^MTP-AUDIT-C05-015]

<a id="mtp-000192"></a>
<!-- pair: MTP-000192; format: prose; role: main_text -->

རྡོ་རྗེ་འཆང་གིས་བཀའ་སྩལ་པ

Vajradhara spoke:[^T05-001][^MTP-AUDIT-C05-014][^MTP-AUDIT-C05-016][^MTP-AUDIT-C05-017]

<a id="mtp-000193"></a>
<!-- pair: MTP-000193; format: verse; role: main_text -->

གསང་བ་མཆོག་བདག་རྡོ་རྗེ་འཛིན  
གཉིས་ནི་གཞི་ངོ་བོ་གཅིག་ཁྱད་པར་ལས  
སྤྲོས་མེད་ཉག་གཅིག་དགོངས་པར་གནས  
བདེ་ཆེན་ཐིག་ལེའི་རང་བཞིན་ཅན  
སངས་རྒྱས་ཀུན་བདག་དཀྱིལ་འཁོར་གཅིག

“Supreme Lord of Secrets, holder of the vajra!  
The two—the Ground, one in essence; from distinction—  
abide in the unique enlightened intent without conceptual elaborations,  
endowed with the intrinsic nature of the sphere of great bliss:  
one mandala, lord of all buddhas.[^T05-001][^T05-003][^CH05-G000651][^MTP-AUDIT-C05-014][^MTP-AUDIT-C05-016][^MTP-AUDIT-C05-018][^MTP-AUDIT-C05-019][^MTP-AUDIT-C05-020][^MTP-AUDIT-C05-021][^MTP-AUDIT-C05-022]

<a id="mtp-000194"></a>
<!-- pair: MTP-000194; format: verse; role: main_text -->

ངོ་བོ་ཉིད་ཀྱི་ཀ་དག་པས  
རང་བཞིན་ཉིད་ལ་ལྷུན་གྲུབ་པོ

Through the primordial purity of essence itself,  
there is spontaneous presence in intrinsic nature itself.[^T05-004][^MTP-AUDIT-C05-014][^MTP-AUDIT-C05-016][^MTP-AUDIT-C05-023][^MTP-AUDIT-C05-024]

<a id="mtp-000195"></a>
<!-- pair: MTP-000195; format: verse; role: main_text -->

ས་དང་ཕྱོགས་དང་རིས་ལས་འདས  
ཡོད་དང་མེད་དང་ལྟར་སྣང་མེད  
གཅིག་དང་གཉིས་དང་བགྲང་ཡུལ་མེད  
སྟོང་པ་ཆེན་པོར་བབས་ཀྱིས་གྲུབ

Beyond grounds, directions, and divisions,  
there is no existence, nonexistence, or seeming appearance;  
there is no one, two, or object of enumeration.  
In great emptiness, [it] is established just as it settles.[^T05-004][^MTP-AUDIT-C05-014][^MTP-AUDIT-C05-016][^MTP-AUDIT-C05-025][^MTP-AUDIT-C05-026][^MTP-AUDIT-C05-027][^MTP-AUDIT-C05-028][^MTP-AUDIT-C05-030][^MTP-AUDIT-C05-031]

<a id="mtp-000196"></a>
<!-- pair: MTP-000196; format: prose; role: source_metadata -->

> [source_metadata] ༤༥༦

> [source_metadata] [Printed page 456.][^MTP-AUDIT-C05-029]

<a id="mtp-000197"></a>
<!-- pair: MTP-000197; format: verse; role: main_text -->

ལུང་དང་རིགས་པའི་སྤྱོད་ཡུལ་མིན  
རྒྱུད་དང་མན་ངག་གིས་མི་མཚོན  
ལྟ་དང་སྒོམ་དང་སྤྱོད་པས་ནི  
དེ་ཡི་དོན་རྣམས་ཤེས་མི་འགྱུར

It is not the domain of scriptural transmission and reasoning;  
tantras and pith instructions do not indicate it.  
Through view, cultivation, and activity,  
its meanings do not become known.[^T05-005][^MTP-AUDIT-C05-028][^MTP-AUDIT-C05-030][^MTP-AUDIT-C05-032][^MTP-AUDIT-C05-033][^MTP-AUDIT-C05-034][^T05-L038]

<a id="mtp-000198"></a>
<!-- pair: MTP-000198; format: verse; role: main_text -->

དེ་དོན་འབྲས་བུ་གྲུབ་པ་མེད  
ཆོས་ཀྱིས་དེ་ཉིད་བསྒྲིབས་པར་འགྱུར  
བསམ་པས་འགྲུབ་པ་མ་ཡིན་ཏེ  
ཤེས་རབ་ཀྱིས་ཀྱང་དཔྱད་དུ་མེད

As for its meaning, there is no accomplishment of a result;  
through phenomena, that itself becomes obscured.  
It is not accomplished through thinking,  
nor can it be examined through discerning knowing.[^T05-005][^MTP-AUDIT-C05-028][^MTP-AUDIT-C05-030][^MTP-AUDIT-C05-035][^MTP-AUDIT-C05-036][^MTP-AUDIT-C05-037][^T05-L002][^T05-L004]

<a id="mtp-000199"></a>
<!-- pair: MTP-000199; format: verse; role: main_text -->

སྦྱིན་དང་ཚུལ་ཁྲིམས་དང་བཟོད་པ་ཡིས  
དེ་ཉིད་གནོད་དང་ཕན་འབྲས་བུ་བྲལ  
ཐོས་དང་བསམ་དང་བསྒོམས་པས་ནི  
མཆོག་ཏུ་བརྟགས་ཀྱང་དོན་མི་མཐོང

Through generosity, ethical discipline, and patience,  
that itself is free from the results of harm and benefit.  
Through hearing, thinking, and cultivation,  
even if [it] is examined supremely, the meaning is not seen.[^T05-006][^MTP-AUDIT-C05-028][^MTP-AUDIT-C05-030][^MTP-AUDIT-C05-038][^MTP-AUDIT-C05-039][^MTP-AUDIT-C05-040][^MTP-AUDIT-C05-041][^T05-L004]

<a id="mtp-000200"></a>
<!-- pair: MTP-000200; format: verse; role: main_text -->

དེ་ལ་དཀྱིལ་འཁོར་ཡོད་པ་མིན  
ལྷ་མེད་སྐྱེད་པའི་ས་བོན་མེད  
སྔགས་དང་ཕྱག་རྒྱས་ཅི་ཞིག་བྱ  
མཆོད་པ་ལ་སོགས་སྤྲོ་མི་དགོས  
དབང་དང་དམ་ཚིག་ག་ལ་ཡོད

There is no mandala in that;  
there is no deity, no seed of generation.  
What would one do with mantra and seals?  
There is no need to elaborate offerings and the like.  
Where are empowerment and sacred pledges?[^T05-007][^MTP-AUDIT-C05-028][^MTP-AUDIT-C05-030][^MTP-AUDIT-C05-042][^MTP-AUDIT-C05-043][^MTP-AUDIT-C05-044][^MTP-AUDIT-C05-045][^MTP-AUDIT-C05-046]

<a id="mtp-000201"></a>
<!-- pair: MTP-000201; format: prose; role: source_metadata -->

> [source_metadata] ༤༥༧

> [source_metadata] [Printed page 457.][^MTP-AUDIT-C05-048]

<a id="mtp-000202"></a>
<!-- pair: MTP-000202; format: verse; role: main_text -->

བསྙེན་པ་དང་ནི་སྒྲུབ་པ་མེད  
འཁོར་ལོ་སྤྲོ་བ་ཡོད་མ་ཡིན  
བསྲུང་བ་མེད་ཅིང་བསྲུང་ཡུལ་མེད  
གདོན་དང་གནོད་པའི་ཡུལ་ལས་འདས

There is no approach or accomplishment;  
there is no elaboration of wheels.  
There is no protection and no object of protection;  
[it] is beyond the domain of obstructing spirits and harm.[^T05-007][^MTP-AUDIT-C05-047][^MTP-AUDIT-C05-049][^MTP-AUDIT-C05-050][^MTP-AUDIT-C05-051][^MTP-AUDIT-C05-052][^MTP-AUDIT-C05-053][^T05-L038]

<a id="mtp-000203"></a>
<!-- pair: MTP-000203; format: verse; role: main_text -->

དེ་ནི་ཡིན་འགྲོ་བའང་ཡིན  
སེམས་ཡིན་སེམས་ལས་བྱུང་བ་སྟེ  
དུ་མ་ཡིན་ལ་ཉག་གཅིག་ཡིན  
འཁོར་དང་མྱང་འདས་ང་རང་ཡིན

It is that, and is also beings;  
it is ordinary mind and what arises from ordinary mind.  
It is many, and it is unique;  
I myself am cyclic existence and transcendence of sorrow.[^T05-008][^MTP-AUDIT-C05-047][^MTP-AUDIT-C05-049][^MTP-AUDIT-C05-054][^MTP-AUDIT-C05-055][^MTP-AUDIT-C05-056]

<a id="mtp-000204"></a>
<!-- pair: MTP-000204; format: verse; role: main_text -->

དཀྱིལ་འཁོར་ལ་སོགས་རིམ་འཇུག་ཡིན  
ཆོས་ཡིན་ཆོས་སུ་སྣང་བ་ཡིན  
གནས་པ་ཡིན་ལ་གྲོལ་བ་ཡིན  
འདུག་དང་ཉལ་དང་འཆག་པ་ཡིན  
སྲོག་ཡིན་གཅོད་པ་ཉིད་ཀྱང་ཡིན

It is gradual entry into mandalas and the like;  
it is phenomena and appears as phenomena.  
It is abiding, and it is liberation;  
it is sitting, lying down, and walking.  
It is life, and it is also cutting [life] off.[^T05-008][^MTP-AUDIT-C05-047][^MTP-AUDIT-C05-049][^MTP-AUDIT-C05-057][^MTP-AUDIT-C05-058][^MTP-AUDIT-C05-059][^MTP-AUDIT-C05-060][^MTP-AUDIT-C05-061][^MTP-AUDIT-C05-062][^MTP-AUDIT-C05-064][^MTP-AUDIT-C05-065][^T05-L001][^T05-L002]

<a id="mtp-000205"></a>
<!-- pair: MTP-000205; format: prose; role: source_metadata -->

> [source_metadata] ༤༥༨

> [source_metadata] [Printed page 458.][^MTP-AUDIT-C05-063]

<a id="mtp-000206"></a>
<!-- pair: MTP-000206; format: verse; role: main_text -->

འཇིག་རྟེན་གསུམ་པོ་དེ་ལས་སྐྱེས  
ལྷ་དང་སྔགས་དང་ཕྱག་རྒྱར་སྣང  
དགེ་དང་དེ་ཡི་འབྲས་ལྟར་བྱུང  
ཕ་རོལ་ཕྱིན་པ་བཅུ་ཡང་དེ

The three worlds arise from that;  
[it] appears as deities, mantra, and seals.  
[It] arises as virtue and its result;  
the ten perfections, too, are that.[^T05-009][^MTP-AUDIT-C05-062][^MTP-AUDIT-C05-064][^MTP-AUDIT-C05-066][^MTP-AUDIT-C05-067][^MTP-AUDIT-C05-068][^T05-L001]

<a id="mtp-000207"></a>
<!-- pair: MTP-000207; format: verse; role: main_text -->

ས་རྣམས་དང་ནི་ཡོན་ཏན་ཀྱང  
དེ་ཉིད་ལས་ནི་ཐམས་ཅད་བྱུང  
སྤྱོད་པ་སྒོམ་པ་ཉིད་དང་ནི  
ལྟ་བ་ལ་སོགས་ཀུན་ཀྱང་བྱུང  
ཆེ་ཆུང་རིམ་པའི་ཐེག་པ་ཡང  
དེ་ལས་ཐམས་ཅད་བྱུང་བའོ

The grounds and the qualities, too,  
all arise from that itself.  
Activity and cultivation itself,  
view and all the rest also arise;  
the vehicles in their greater and lesser stages, too,  
all arise from that.[^T05-009][^MTP-AUDIT-C05-062][^MTP-AUDIT-C05-064][^MTP-AUDIT-C05-069][^MTP-AUDIT-C05-070][^MTP-AUDIT-C05-071][^MTP-AUDIT-C05-072][^MTP-AUDIT-C05-073]

<a id="mtp-000208"></a>
<!-- pair: MTP-000208; format: verse; role: main_text -->

མདོར་ན་ཇི་ལྟར་སྣང་བ་ཡི  
ས་ཆུ་མེ་རླུང་ནམ་མཁའ་དང  
འབྱུང་བ་ཆེ་རྣམས་དེ་ལས་བྱུང

In brief, however they appear,  
earth, water, fire, wind, and space—  
the great elements—arise from that.[^T05-010][^MTP-AUDIT-C05-062][^MTP-AUDIT-C05-064][^MTP-AUDIT-C05-074][^MTP-AUDIT-C05-075]

<a id="mtp-000209"></a>
<!-- pair: MTP-000209; format: verse; role: main_text -->

ལྷ་དང་ལྷ་མིན་མི་རྣམས་དང  
དམྱལ་བ་བྱོལ་སོང་ཡི་དྭགས་དང

Gods, demigods, and humans,  
hell beings, animals, and hungry ghosts[^T05-010][^MTP-AUDIT-C05-062][^MTP-AUDIT-C05-064][^MTP-AUDIT-C05-076][^MTP-AUDIT-C05-078][^T05-L025]

<a id="mtp-000210"></a>
<!-- pair: MTP-000210; format: prose; role: source_metadata -->

> [source_metadata] ༤༥༩

> [source_metadata] [Printed page 459.][^MTP-AUDIT-C05-077]

<a id="mtp-000211"></a>
<!-- pair: MTP-000211; format: verse; role: main_text -->

ཆེན་པོ་དེ་ལས་ཡང་དག་བྱུང

arise authentically from that great [one].[^T05-010][^MTP-AUDIT-C05-076][^MTP-AUDIT-C05-078][^MTP-AUDIT-C05-079]

<a id="mtp-000212"></a>
<!-- pair: MTP-000212; format: verse; role: main_text -->

འདོད་ཆགས་ཞེ་སྡང་གཏི་མུག་དང  
ང་རྒྱལ་ཕྲག་དོག་ལ་སོགས་ནི  
དེ་ཉིད་ལས་ནི་ཆོ་འཕྲུལ་འབྱུང

Desire, hatred, deluded dullness,  
pride, jealousy, and the like—  
magical display arises from that itself.[^T05-010][^MTP-AUDIT-C05-076][^MTP-AUDIT-C05-078][^MTP-AUDIT-C05-080]

<a id="mtp-000213"></a>
<!-- pair: MTP-000213; format: verse; role: main_text -->

མ་རིག་སེམས་དང་ཡིད་རྣམས་དང  
ཕུང་པོ་ཁམས་དང་སྐྱེ་མཆེད་དང  
རྣམ་པར་ཤེས་པའི་ཚོགས་རྣམས་དང  
དབང་པོ་ལྔ་ལ་སོགས་པ་བྱུང

Ignorance, ordinary mind, and mental faculties,  
aggregates, elements, and sense bases,  
the collections of consciousness,  
the five faculties and the like arise.[^T05-011][^MTP-AUDIT-C05-076][^MTP-AUDIT-C05-078][^MTP-AUDIT-C05-081][^MTP-AUDIT-C05-082][^MTP-AUDIT-C05-083]

<a id="mtp-000214"></a>
<!-- pair: MTP-000214; format: verse; role: main_text -->

སྒྲ་དང་དྲི་དང་རོ་དང་ནི  
རེག་བྱ་ཉིད་དང་འདོད་ཡོན་ནི  
དེ་ཉིད་ཀྱི་ནི་རྒྱན་དུ་བྱུང

Sound, smell, taste,  
objects of touch, and objects of desire  
arise as its adornment.[^T05-012][^MTP-AUDIT-C05-076][^MTP-AUDIT-C05-078][^MTP-AUDIT-C05-084][^MTP-AUDIT-C05-085]

<a id="mtp-000215"></a>
<!-- pair: MTP-000215; format: verse; role: main_text -->

གསོག་དང་ཡ་མ་བརླ་དང་ནི  
སྙིང་པོ་མེད་ལ་སོགས་པ་ཡང  
དེ་ཡི་རྩལ་དང་རོལ་པར་བྱུང

Hollowness and insubstantiality,  
lack of a core and the like, too,  
arise as its expressiveness and play.[^T05-013][^CH05-G000721][^MTP-AUDIT-C05-076][^MTP-AUDIT-C05-078][^MTP-AUDIT-C05-086][^MTP-AUDIT-C05-087][^MTP-AUDIT-C05-088]

<a id="mtp-000216"></a>
<!-- pair: MTP-000216; format: verse; role: main_text -->

ཀུན་གཞི་དང་ནི་ཉེར་ལེན་གྱི  
ཤ་དང་ཁྲག་དང་ཆུ་སེར་དང  
རུས་པ་པགས་པ་བ་སྤུ་དང  
བསགས་པའི་ལུས་རྣམས་དེ་ལས་བྱུང

The all-basis and the appropriated  
flesh, blood, lymph,  
bones, skin, and body hair—  
the assembled bodies arise from that.[^T05-014][^MTP-AUDIT-C05-076][^MTP-AUDIT-C05-078][^MTP-AUDIT-C05-089][^MTP-AUDIT-C05-090][^MTP-AUDIT-C05-091][^MTP-AUDIT-C05-092]

<a id="mtp-000217"></a>
<!-- pair: MTP-000217; format: verse; role: main_text -->

ལུས་དང་ངག་དང་ཡིད་དང་ནི  
སེམས་བྱུང་ཉོན་མོངས་སྣ་ཚོགས་ནི  
ཐམས་ཅད་ང་ཡི་ངོ་ལས་བྱུང

Body, speech, and mental faculty,  
mental factors and the various afflictions—  
all arise from my own identity.[^T05-015][^MTP-AUDIT-C05-076][^MTP-AUDIT-C05-078][^MTP-AUDIT-C05-093][^MTP-AUDIT-C05-094][^MTP-AUDIT-C05-095][^MTP-AUDIT-C05-097][^MTP-AUDIT-C05-098]

<a id="mtp-000218"></a>
<!-- pair: MTP-000218; format: prose; role: source_metadata -->

> [source_metadata] ༤༦༠

> [source_metadata] [Printed page 460.][^MTP-AUDIT-C05-096]

<a id="mtp-000219"></a>
<!-- pair: MTP-000219; format: verse; role: main_text -->

སྐྱེས་པ་བུད་མེད་རྟ་གླང་དང  
གཟུགས་ནི་ཐམས་ཅད་དེ་ལས་བྱུང  
བཟའ་དང་ཉལ་དང་འདུག་པ་དང  
སྣ་ཚོགས་ལས་ནི་བྱུང་བའོ

Men, women, horses, and cattle—  
all forms arise from that.  
Eating, lying down, and sitting,  
the various actions, arise.[^T05-015][^MTP-AUDIT-C05-095][^MTP-AUDIT-C05-097][^MTP-AUDIT-C05-099][^MTP-AUDIT-C05-100][^MTP-AUDIT-C05-101][^T05-L005][^T05-L006]

<a id="mtp-000220"></a>
<!-- pair: MTP-000220; format: verse; role: main_text -->

ཀྱེ་ཁྲོ་བདག་དེ་ལྟར་གནས་པའི་ཆོས་ཉིད་ལ  
འཁྲུལ་པ་གདོད་ནས་མེད་པ་སྟེ  
ཆོས་སྐུ་ནམ་མཁའ་ལྟ་བུ་ལ  
གློ་བུར་སེམས་ཅན་སྤྲིན་གྱིས་བསྒྲིབས

O lord of wrathful ones, in the nature of phenomena abiding in this way,  
there is no delusion from the beginning.  
The dharma embodiment, like space,  
is obscured by adventitious clouds of karmic beings.[^T05-001][^T05-016][^MTP-AUDIT-C05-095][^MTP-AUDIT-C05-097][^MTP-AUDIT-C05-102][^MTP-AUDIT-C05-103]

<a id="mtp-000221"></a>
<!-- pair: MTP-000221; format: verse; role: main_text -->

མ་འཁྲུལ་པ་ཡི་ཆོས་ཉིད་ཀྱང  
གློ་བུར་འཁྲུལ་པའི་ཚུལ་དང་ལྡན  
རྒྱུ་རྐྱེན་དང་བཅས་སྐད་ཅིག་མ  
ཡུལ་ལ་དེ་ལྟར་སྣང་མོད་ཀྱང

Even the undeluded nature of phenomena  
is endowed with an adventitious manner of delusion.  
Momentary, together with causes and conditions,  
although it appears thus with regard to objects,[^T05-016][^MTP-AUDIT-C05-095][^MTP-AUDIT-C05-097][^MTP-AUDIT-C05-104][^MTP-AUDIT-C05-105][^MTP-AUDIT-C05-106][^MTP-AUDIT-C05-107][^T05-L003]

<a id="mtp-000222"></a>
<!-- pair: MTP-000222; format: verse; role: main_text -->

ཐོག་མ་ཉིད་ཀྱི་ཀ་དག་ལ  
འཁྲུལ་ཞེས་ཙམ་དུ་བརྗོད་དུ་མེད  
དེ་བཞིན་མ་འཁྲུལ་པ་ཡང་ཅི  
དེ་ཕྱིར་འཁྲུལ་པ་གདོད་ནས་དག

in the primordial purity of the very beginning,  
one cannot even say “delusion.”  
Likewise, what could “undeluded” be?  
Therefore, delusion is pure from the beginning.[^T05-017][^MTP-AUDIT-C05-095][^MTP-AUDIT-C05-097][^MTP-AUDIT-C05-108][^MTP-AUDIT-C05-109][^MTP-AUDIT-C05-110]

<a id="mtp-000223"></a>
<!-- pair: MTP-000223; format: verse; role: main_text -->

གཞི་སྣང་ཆེན་པོ་དེ་ཉིད་ལ  
མ་རིག་པ་ཞེས་བརྟག་མེད་པས

In that great Ground-appearance itself,  
because there is no designation as “ignorance,”[^T05-017][^MTP-AUDIT-C05-095][^MTP-AUDIT-C05-097][^MTP-AUDIT-C05-111][^MTP-AUDIT-C05-112][^MTP-AUDIT-C05-113][^MTP-AUDIT-C05-115][^MTP-AUDIT-C05-116]

<a id="mtp-000224"></a>
<!-- pair: MTP-000224; format: prose; role: source_metadata -->

> [source_metadata] ༤༦༡

> [source_metadata] [Printed page 461.][^MTP-AUDIT-C05-114]

<a id="mtp-000225"></a>
<!-- pair: MTP-000225; format: verse; role: main_text -->

འཁྲུལ་པར་གྲུབ་པ་ཡོད་མ་ཡིན

there is no establishment as delusion.[^T05-017][^MTP-AUDIT-C05-113][^MTP-AUDIT-C05-115]

<a id="mtp-000226"></a>
<!-- pair: MTP-000226; format: verse; role: main_text -->

བློ་ཞེས་ཀུན་ཏུ་མ་བརྟགས་པས  
དྲི་མ་འཁྲུལ་པའི་མིང་ཡང་མེད  
མིང་དང་ཡི་གེའི་ཚོགས་མེད་པས  
བཏགས་པའི་འཁྲུལ་པ་ཡོད་མ་ཡིན

Because “conceptual mind” is not imputed,  
there is not even the name of stain or delusion.  
Without the gathering of names and letters,  
there is no imputed delusion.[^T05-017][^MTP-AUDIT-C05-113][^MTP-AUDIT-C05-115][^MTP-AUDIT-C05-117][^MTP-AUDIT-C05-118][^MTP-AUDIT-C05-119]

<a id="mtp-000227"></a>
<!-- pair: MTP-000227; format: verse; role: main_text -->

ཆོས་ཞེས་མིང་དུ་དུམ་གྲགས་པས  
བློ་བཏགས་འཁྲུལ་པ་མིང་ཡང་མེད  
སེམས་དང་ཡིད་དུ་མ་གྲུབ་པས  
འགྱུ་བྱེད་འཁྲུལ་པ་ངང་གིས་མེད

Because, as the name “phenomena,” [retained: དུམ་གྲགས་པས],  
there is not even the name of delusion imputed by the conceptual mind.  
Because it is not established as ordinary mind and mental faculty,  
the delusion that brings about movement is naturally absent.[^T05-018][^CH05-G000756][^MTP-AUDIT-C05-113][^MTP-AUDIT-C05-115][^MTP-AUDIT-C05-120][^MTP-AUDIT-C05-121][^MTP-AUDIT-C05-122][^MTP-AUDIT-C05-123][^T05-L002]

<a id="mtp-000228"></a>
<!-- pair: MTP-000228; format: verse; role: main_text -->

ཕྲ་རགས་དྲི་མ་གཉིས་མེད་པས  
འབྱུང་རྐྱེན་འཁྲུལ་པ་ངང་གིས་མེད  
བྱ་དང་བྱེད་པ་གཉིས་མེད་པས  
ཡུལ་འཛིན་འཁྲུལ་པ་ག་ལ་ཡོད

Because there are no two stains, subtle and coarse,  
delusion as a condition of arising is naturally absent.  
Because doing and the doer are not two,  
where is the delusion of apprehending objects?[^T05-019][^MTP-AUDIT-C05-113][^MTP-AUDIT-C05-115][^MTP-AUDIT-C05-124][^MTP-AUDIT-C05-125][^MTP-AUDIT-C05-126][^MTP-AUDIT-C05-127][^T05-L007][^T05-L008]

<a id="mtp-000229"></a>
<!-- pair: MTP-000229; format: verse; role: main_text -->

ཡུལ་དང་དབང་པོ་གཉིས་མེད་པས  
ཡུལ་འཛིན་འཁྲུལ་པ་མེད་པའོ

Because objects and faculties are not two,  
there is no delusion of apprehending objects.[^T05-019][^MTP-AUDIT-C05-113][^MTP-AUDIT-C05-115][^MTP-AUDIT-C05-128][^MTP-AUDIT-C05-129][^T05-L003]

<a id="mtp-000230"></a>
<!-- pair: MTP-000230; format: verse; role: main_text -->

ཀྱེ་ཁྲོ་བདག་ཆེན་པོ་ལེགས་པར་ཉོན  
རང་བཞིན་སྣང་བའི་ཆོས་རྣམས་ལ

O great lord of wrathful ones, listen well!  
In the phenomena appearing as intrinsic nature,[^T05-001][^T05-019][^MTP-AUDIT-C05-113][^MTP-AUDIT-C05-115][^MTP-AUDIT-C05-130][^MTP-AUDIT-C05-131][^MTP-AUDIT-C05-132][^MTP-AUDIT-C05-134][^MTP-AUDIT-C05-135][^T05-L002]

<a id="mtp-000231"></a>
<!-- pair: MTP-000231; format: prose; role: source_metadata -->

> [source_metadata] ༤༦༢

> [source_metadata] [Printed page 462.][^MTP-AUDIT-C05-133]

<a id="mtp-000232"></a>
<!-- pair: MTP-000232; format: verse; role: main_text -->

དྲི་མ་མེད་པས་ཕྲ་རགས་ལོག  
གཟུང་འཛིན་མེད་པས་འཁྲུལ་པ་ལོག  
འགྱུ་བ་མེད་པས་ལས་རླུང་ལོག  
ཟང་ཐལ་ཡིན་པས་དངོས་པོ་ལོག

because there are no stains, subtle and coarse are reversed;  
because there is no apprehended object and apprehending subject, delusion is reversed.  
Because there is no movement, karmic wind is reversed;  
because there is unimpeded penetration, entities are reversed.[^T05-020][^MTP-AUDIT-C05-132][^MTP-AUDIT-C05-134][^MTP-AUDIT-C05-136][^MTP-AUDIT-C05-137][^MTP-AUDIT-C05-138][^T05-L009][^T05-L010]

<a id="mtp-000233"></a>
<!-- pair: MTP-000233; format: verse; role: main_text -->

གསལ་བ་ཡིན་ཕྱིར་སྟོང་པ་ལོག  
ཡེ་ནས་རྫོགས་པས་བསྐྱེད་རིམ་ལོག  
རྩོལ་བ་མེད་པས་སྤྱོད་པ་ལོག  
མ་བཙལ་བཞག་པས་བསྒོམ་པ་ལོག

Because there is clarity, emptiness is reversed;  
because [it] is complete from the beginning, the generation stage is reversed.  
Because there is no exertion, activity is reversed;  
because [it] is left without seeking, cultivation is reversed.[^T05-020][^MTP-AUDIT-C05-132][^MTP-AUDIT-C05-134][^MTP-AUDIT-C05-139][^MTP-AUDIT-C05-140][^MTP-AUDIT-C05-141][^MTP-AUDIT-C05-142][^T05-L011]

<a id="mtp-000234"></a>
<!-- pair: MTP-000234; format: verse; role: main_text -->

རང་གྲོལ་ཡིན་པས་སྣང་བ་ལོག  
མངོན་དུ་སྣང་བས་ལྟ་བ་ལོག  
སྒྲ་ཚིག་མེད་པས་ཐ་སྙད་ལོག  
ཡོད་མ་མྱོང་པས་ཞེན་པ་ལོག

Because there is self-liberation, appearance is reversed;  
because [it] appears manifestly, view is reversed.  
Because there are no words or phrases, designation is reversed;  
because [it] has never existed, clinging is reversed.[^T05-020][^MTP-AUDIT-C05-132][^MTP-AUDIT-C05-134][^MTP-AUDIT-C05-143][^MTP-AUDIT-C05-144][^MTP-AUDIT-C05-145][^MTP-AUDIT-C05-146]

<a id="mtp-000235"></a>
<!-- pair: MTP-000235; format: verse; role: main_text -->

མེད་མ་མྱོང་བས་གཞན་ལྟོས་ལོག  
གཟུང་དུ་མེད་པས་བློས་བཏགས་ལོག  
ཉག་གཅིག་ཡིན་པས་གྲངས་མཐའ་ལོག

Because [it] has never been nonexistent, dependence on another is reversed;  
because there is nothing to apprehend, conceptual-mind imputation is reversed.  
Because [it] is unique, the limits of number are reversed.[^T05-020][^MTP-AUDIT-C05-132][^MTP-AUDIT-C05-134][^MTP-AUDIT-C05-147][^MTP-AUDIT-C05-148][^MTP-AUDIT-C05-149][^MTP-AUDIT-C05-272]

<a id="mtp-000236"></a>
<!-- pair: MTP-000236; format: prose; role: source_metadata -->

> [source_metadata] ༤༦༣

> [source_metadata] [Printed page 463.][^MTP-AUDIT-C05-271][^MTP-AUDIT-C05-273]

<a id="mtp-000237"></a>
<!-- pair: MTP-000237; format: verse; role: main_text -->

ཡོངས་ལ་ཁྱབ་པས་གཞན་འབྱུང་འགགས  
རང་ཤར་ཡིན་པས་མུན་པ་སངས  
སྙིང་པོ་ཡིན་པས་ཆོས་སྐུར་ཁྱབ  
གསལ་བར་སྣང་བས་ལོངས་སྐུར་རྒྱས

Because [it] pervades all, arising from another ceases;  
because there is self-arising, darkness clears.  
Because [it] is the core, [it] pervades as dharma embodiment;  
because [it] appears clearly, [it] expands as complete enjoyment embodiment.[^T05-021][^MTP-AUDIT-C05-150][^MTP-AUDIT-C05-151][^MTP-AUDIT-C05-152][^MTP-AUDIT-C05-153][^MTP-AUDIT-C05-272]

<a id="mtp-000238"></a>
<!-- pair: MTP-000238; format: verse; role: main_text -->

མ་བུ་འཕྲད་པས་སྤྲུལ་སྐུར་བྱུང  
རྟགས་སུ་ཡོད་པས་ཐབས་སུ་བྱུང  
བསམ་འདས་ཡིན་པས་དྲན་པ་སངས  
རྟག་ཆད་མེད་པས་གྲུབ་མཐའ་རྫོགས

Because mother and child meet, [it] arises as emanation embodiment;  
because [it] exists as a sign, [it] arises as means.  
Because [it] is beyond thinking, mindfulness clears;  
because there is no permanence or annihilation, tenet systems are complete.[^T05-021][^MTP-AUDIT-C05-154][^MTP-AUDIT-C05-155][^MTP-AUDIT-C05-156][^MTP-AUDIT-C05-157][^MTP-AUDIT-C05-272][^T05-L012]

<a id="mtp-000239"></a>
<!-- pair: MTP-000239; format: verse; role: main_text -->

བཅུང་དུ་དྲིལ་བས་རིག་པ་གསལ  
རང་གྲོལ་ཡོད་པས་ཅོག་བཞག་དང  
དེ་ཉིད་ལོག་པའི་རིག་པ་ནི  
རང་ལས་བྱུང་བས་གྲོལ་ཞེས་བྱ

By gathering into [retained: བཅུང], awareness is clear;  
because there is self-liberation, [there is] leaving as it is, and  
the awareness of that very reversal,  
because it arises from itself, is called “liberated.”[^T05-022][^CH05-G000793][^MTP-AUDIT-C05-158][^MTP-AUDIT-C05-159][^MTP-AUDIT-C05-160][^MTP-AUDIT-C05-161][^MTP-AUDIT-C05-272][^MTP-AUDIT-C05-275]

<a id="mtp-000240"></a>
<!-- pair: MTP-000240; format: prose; role: source_metadata -->

> [source_metadata] ༤༦༤

> [source_metadata] [Printed page 464.][^MTP-AUDIT-C05-274][^MTP-AUDIT-C05-276]

<a id="mtp-000241"></a>
<!-- pair: MTP-000241; format: verse; role: main_text -->

གནས་པ་ཡིན་པས་ཁྱབ་ཅེས་བྱ  
མཚན་མ་མེད་པས་སྟོང་ཞེས་བྱ  
ནུབ་པ་མེད་པས་ཤར་ཞེས་བྱ  
རྡོས་བཅས་མེད་པས་ཟང་ཐལ་ལོ

Because [it] abides, [it] is called “pervading”;  
because there are no marks, [it] is called “empty.”  
Because there is no setting, [it] is called “arisen”;  
because there is no material encumbrance, [there is] unimpeded penetration.[^T05-023][^MTP-AUDIT-C05-162][^MTP-AUDIT-C05-163][^MTP-AUDIT-C05-164][^MTP-AUDIT-C05-165][^MTP-AUDIT-C05-275]

<a id="mtp-000242"></a>
<!-- pair: MTP-000242; format: verse; role: main_text -->

གྲོལ་བར་གནས་པས་ལོག་ཅེས་བྱ  
སྣ་ཚོགས་དངས་པས་སངས་ཞེས་བྱ  
གཉིས་སུ་མེད་པས་གཅིག་པུ་ཉིད  
ཐམས་ཅད་བསྐྱེད་པས་རྒྱུ་ཞེས་བྱ

Because [it] abides liberated, [it] is called “reversed”;  
because variety has cleared, [it] is called “purified.”  
Because [it] is not two, [it] is the single one;  
because [it] generates everything, [it] is called “cause.”[^T05-023][^CH05-G000803][^MTP-AUDIT-C05-166][^MTP-AUDIT-C05-167][^MTP-AUDIT-C05-168][^MTP-AUDIT-C05-169][^MTP-AUDIT-C05-275][^T05-L037]

<a id="mtp-000243"></a>
<!-- pair: MTP-000243; format: verse; role: main_text -->

མ་ལུས་རྫོགས་པས་འབྲས་བུ་ཉིད  
དམིགས་པ་དག་པས་ལམ་ཞེས་བྱ  
འཁྲུལ་པ་ཟད་པས་ལུང་ཡང་ཡིན  
ཡེ་ནས་གནས་པས་རྒྱུད་ཅེས་བྱ  
མཚོན་དུ་ཡོད་པས་མན་ངག་ཡིན  
གྲངས་ལས་འདས་པས་རྩིས་ཞེས་བྱ

Because [it] is complete without remainder, [it] is the result itself;  
because objects of focus are pure, [it] is called “path.”  
Because delusion is exhausted, [it] is also transmission;  
because [it] abides primordially, [it] is called “continuum.”  
Because [it] can be indicated, [it] is pith instruction;  
because [it] is beyond number, [it] is called “reckoning.”[^T05-023][^MTP-AUDIT-C05-170][^MTP-AUDIT-C05-171][^MTP-AUDIT-C05-172][^MTP-AUDIT-C05-173][^MTP-AUDIT-C05-174][^MTP-AUDIT-C05-175][^MTP-AUDIT-C05-275]

<a id="mtp-000244"></a>
<!-- pair: MTP-000244; format: verse; role: main_text -->

དེ་ལྟ་བུ་ཡི་སེམས་ཉིད་ནི  
དགོས་འདོད་སྐོངས་པས་ནོར་བུ་འདྲ

Such ordinary mind itself  
is like a jewel, fulfilling needs and desires;[^T05-024][^MTP-AUDIT-C05-176][^MTP-AUDIT-C05-177][^MTP-AUDIT-C05-275][^MTP-AUDIT-C05-278][^T05-L022]

<a id="mtp-000245"></a>
<!-- pair: MTP-000245; format: prose; role: source_metadata -->

> [source_metadata] ༤༦༥

> [source_metadata] [Printed page 465.][^MTP-AUDIT-C05-277][^MTP-AUDIT-C05-279]

<a id="mtp-000246"></a>
<!-- pair: MTP-000246; format: verse; role: main_text -->

དགོས་པ་ཚང་བས་བང་མཛོད་བཞིན  
གང་བསྟན་སྣང་ས་མེ་ལོང་འདྲ  
དྲི་མེད་གསལ་བའི་ཤེལ་སྒོང་འདྲ  
སྣང་བ་སྣ་ཚོགས་ཟ་འོག་གུར

like a treasury, containing all that is needed;  
like a mirror, where whatever is shown appears;  
like a stainless, clear crystal orb;  
various appearances—a brocade tent.[^T05-024][^MTP-AUDIT-C05-178][^MTP-AUDIT-C05-179][^MTP-AUDIT-C05-180][^MTP-AUDIT-C05-181][^MTP-AUDIT-C05-278][^T05-L022]

<a id="mtp-000247"></a>
<!-- pair: MTP-000247; format: verse; role: main_text -->

སྤྲོ་བསྡུ་མེད་པས་ཁྱུང་ཆེན་འདྲ  
ལྟ་བའི་རྩལ་རྫོགས་སེང་གེ་བཞིན  
གཏིང་རྫོགས་ཁྱབ་པས་རྒྱ་མཚོ་འདྲ  
རང་བྱུང་གྲོལ་བས་ནམ་མཁའ་བཞིན  
ཐམས་ཅད་བརྟེན་པས་ས་གཞི་འདྲ  
ཡུལ་རྣམས་ཀུན་ལས་ཁྱད་པར་འཕགས

Because there is no projection or gathering, [it] is like a great garuḍa;  
complete in the expressiveness of view, [it] is like a lion.  
Because [it] pervades with complete depth, [it] is like the ocean;  
naturally arising and liberated, [it] is like space.  
Because everything rests upon [it], [it] is like the earth;  
[it] is exceptionally exalted above all objects.[^T05-024][^MTP-AUDIT-C05-182][^MTP-AUDIT-C05-183][^MTP-AUDIT-C05-184][^MTP-AUDIT-C05-185][^MTP-AUDIT-C05-186][^MTP-AUDIT-C05-187][^MTP-AUDIT-C05-278][^T05-L014]

<a id="mtp-000248"></a>
<!-- pair: MTP-000248; format: verse; role: main_text -->

འགག་པ་མེད་པའི་རིག་པ་ལས  
འཁྲུལ་པ་མེད་པས་རྟོག་པ་མེད  
ཡེ་གྲོལ་ཡིན་པས་གཏན་ནས་འཕགས  
རང་གྲོལ་ཡིན་པས་ཡུལ་རྐྱེན་ཟད  
ཅེར་གྲོལ་ཡིན་པས་སྣང་བ་དག

Within awareness without cessation,  
because there is no delusion, there is no conceptual thought.  
Because there is primordial liberation, [it] is utterly exalted;  
because there is self-liberation, object-conditions are exhausted.  
Because there is naked liberation, appearances are pure.[^T05-025][^MTP-AUDIT-C05-188][^MTP-AUDIT-C05-189][^MTP-AUDIT-C05-190][^MTP-AUDIT-C05-191][^MTP-AUDIT-C05-192][^MTP-AUDIT-C05-278][^MTP-AUDIT-C05-281][^T05-L014][^T05-L023]

<a id="mtp-000249"></a>
<!-- pair: MTP-000249; format: prose; role: source_metadata -->

> [source_metadata] ༤༦༦

> [source_metadata] [Printed page 466.][^MTP-AUDIT-C05-280][^MTP-AUDIT-C05-282]

<a id="mtp-000250"></a>
<!-- pair: MTP-000250; format: verse; role: main_text -->

མཐའ་གྲོལ་ཡིན་པས་མུ་བཞི་འགགས  
གཅིག་གྲོལ་ཡིན་པས་དུ་མ་སྟོངས

Because there is liberation from extremes, the four alternatives cease;  
because there is liberation of one, the many are emptied.[^T05-025][^MTP-AUDIT-C05-193][^MTP-AUDIT-C05-281]

<a id="mtp-000251"></a>
<!-- pair: MTP-000251; format: verse; role: main_text -->

རྐྱེན་རྣམས་རྐྱེན་གྱིས་གྲོལ་བས་ན  
དམིགས་པའི་ཆོས་ལ་ང་མི་ལྟོས  
ཡུལ་རྣམས་ཡུལ་གྱིས་གྲོལ་བས་ན  
གཟུང་འཛིན་གཉིས་ལ་ང་མི་ལྟོས

Since conditions are liberated through conditions,  
I do not depend on phenomena that are objects of focus.  
Since objects are liberated through objects,  
I do not depend on the two, apprehended object and apprehending subject.[^T05-026][^MTP-AUDIT-C05-194][^MTP-AUDIT-C05-195][^MTP-AUDIT-C05-196][^MTP-AUDIT-C05-197][^MTP-AUDIT-C05-281][^T05-L013][^T05-L015]

<a id="mtp-000252"></a>
<!-- pair: MTP-000252; format: verse; role: main_text -->

རྒྱུ་ཉིད་རྒྱུ་ཡིས་གྲོལ་བ་ལ  
འཁོར་འདས་གཉིས་ལང་མི་ལྟོས  
ཆོས་ནི་ཆོས་ཀྱིས་གྲོལ་བ་ལ  
ཐ་སྙད་ཚིག་ལ་ང་མི་ལྟོས

When the cause itself is liberated through the cause,  
[there is] no dependence on cyclic existence and transcendence of sorrow, the two—[retained: ལང].  
When phenomena are liberated through phenomena,  
I do not depend on phrases of designation.[^T05-026][^CH05-G000838][^MTP-AUDIT-C05-198][^MTP-AUDIT-C05-199][^MTP-AUDIT-C05-200][^MTP-AUDIT-C05-201][^MTP-AUDIT-C05-281][^T05-L013][^T05-L015][^T05-L016][^T05-L039]

<a id="mtp-000253"></a>
<!-- pair: MTP-000253; format: verse; role: main_text -->

སེམས་ཉིད་སེམས་ཀྱིས་གྲོལ་བ་ལ  
མཚོན་བྱེད་བློ་ལ་ང་མི་ལྟོས  
དྲི་མས་དྲི་མ་འཁྲུལ་བ་བཞིན  
དེ་ནི་དག་པས་དག་པ་གྲོལ

When ordinary mind itself is liberated through ordinary mind,  
I do not depend on the conceptual mind that indicates.  
Just as stain is deluded through stain,  
that purity is liberated through purity.[^T05-026][^MTP-AUDIT-C05-202][^MTP-AUDIT-C05-203][^MTP-AUDIT-C05-204][^MTP-AUDIT-C05-281][^T05-L009]

<a id="mtp-000254"></a>
<!-- pair: MTP-000254; format: verse; role: main_text -->

དུག་གིས་དུག་རྣམས་འཛིམས་པ་དང  
ལྕགས་ཀྱིས་ལྕགས་རྣམས་གཤོག་པ་དང

As poisons are neutralized by poison,  
iron is cleaved by iron,[^T05-027][^MTP-AUDIT-C05-205][^MTP-AUDIT-C05-281][^MTP-AUDIT-C05-284]

<a id="mtp-000255"></a>
<!-- pair: MTP-000255; format: prose; role: source_metadata -->

> [source_metadata] ༤༦༧

> [source_metadata] [Printed page 467.][^MTP-AUDIT-C05-283][^MTP-AUDIT-C05-285]

<a id="mtp-000256"></a>
<!-- pair: MTP-000256; format: verse; role: main_text -->

རྡོ་ཡིས་རྡོ་རྣམས་གཅོག་པ་དང  
ཤིང་གིས་ཤིང་རྣམས་བསྲེག་པ་ལྟར  
རང་རང་དགྲ་ནི་རང་གིས་བྱེད  
རིགས་མི་མཐུན་པས་གྲོལ་བ་མིན

stones are broken by stone,  
and wood is burned by wood,  
each makes its own enemy itself;  
liberation does not come through an incompatible kind.[^T05-027][^MTP-AUDIT-C05-206][^MTP-AUDIT-C05-207][^MTP-AUDIT-C05-208][^MTP-AUDIT-C05-284][^T05-L013]

<a id="mtp-000257"></a>
<!-- pair: MTP-000257; format: verse; role: main_text -->

དེ་ཕྱིར་གཞི་གནས་རིག་པ་ལ  
རང་ཤར་ཡིན་ཕྱིར་སྣང་བ་རྫོགས  
རང་བྱུང་ཡིན་ཕྱིར་གཞི་ཉིད་རྫོགས  
རང་སྣང་ཡིན་ཕྱིར་ཡུལ་ཡང་རྫོགས

Therefore, in awareness abiding in the Ground,  
because there is self-arising, appearance is complete;  
because there is natural arising, the Ground itself is complete;  
because there is self-appearance, objects too are complete.[^T05-028][^MTP-AUDIT-C05-209][^MTP-AUDIT-C05-210][^MTP-AUDIT-C05-211][^MTP-AUDIT-C05-212][^MTP-AUDIT-C05-284][^T05-L013]

<a id="mtp-000258"></a>
<!-- pair: MTP-000258; format: verse; role: main_text -->

རང་གྲོལ་ཡིན་ཕྱིར་འཁྲུལ་གཞི་རྫོགས  
རང་དག་ཡིན་ཕྱིར་ལམ་ཀུན་རྫོགས  
རང་རིག་ཡིན་ཕྱིར་ཤེས་བྱ་རྫོགས  
རང་ཁྱབ་ཡིན་ཕྱིར་འབྲས་བུ་རྫོགས

Because there is self-liberation, the Ground of delusion is complete;  
because there is self-purification, all paths are complete.  
Because there is self-awareness, what is to be known is complete;  
because there is self-pervasion, the result is complete.[^T05-028][^MTP-AUDIT-C05-213][^MTP-AUDIT-C05-214][^MTP-AUDIT-C05-215][^MTP-AUDIT-C05-284][^T05-L017]

<a id="mtp-000259"></a>
<!-- pair: MTP-000259; format: verse; role: main_text -->

རང་ས་ཡིན་ཕྱིར་རྒྱུ་རྐྱེན་རྫོགས  
རང་གིས་རྟོགས་ཕྱིར་ཆོས་ཉིད་རྫོགས  
རང་ལོག་ཡིན་ཕྱིར་འཁོར་འདས་རྫོགས  
རང་གནས་ཡིན་ཕྱིར་རྒྱུད་ལུང་རྫོགས  
རང་རྫོགས་ཡིན་ཕྱིར་དུས་གཅིག་རྫོགས

Because [it] is its own place, causes and conditions are complete;  
because [it] is realized by itself, the nature of phenomena is complete.  
Because there is self-reversal, cyclic existence and transcendence of sorrow are complete;  
because there is self-abiding, tantras and transmissions are complete.  
Because there is self-completeness, [it] is complete at one time.[^T05-028][^MTP-AUDIT-C05-216][^MTP-AUDIT-C05-217][^MTP-AUDIT-C05-218][^MTP-AUDIT-C05-284][^MTP-AUDIT-C05-287][^T05-L013][^T05-L016][^T05-L018]

<a id="mtp-000260"></a>
<!-- pair: MTP-000260; format: prose; role: source_metadata -->

> [source_metadata] ༤༦༨

> [source_metadata] [Printed page 468.][^MTP-AUDIT-C05-286][^MTP-AUDIT-C05-288]

<a id="mtp-000261"></a>
<!-- pair: MTP-000261; format: verse; role: main_text -->

རང་ཤེས་ཡིན་ཕྱིར་གདམས་ངག་རྫོགས  
རང་བཞག་ཡིན་ཕྱིར་བསམ་གཏན་རྫོགས  
རང་གནས་ཡིན་ཕྱིར་དབྱིངས་རྫོགས་པའོ

Because there is self-knowing, oral instructions are complete;  
because there is self-resting, meditative stability is complete;  
because there is self-abiding, basic space is complete.[^T05-028][^MTP-AUDIT-C05-219][^MTP-AUDIT-C05-220][^MTP-AUDIT-C05-287]

<a id="mtp-000262"></a>
<!-- pair: MTP-000262; format: verse; role: main_text -->

ཨེ་མ་ཧོ  
རང་རྫོགས་པ་ཆེན་པོའི་ཆོས་སྐུ་ནི  
ཀ་ནས་དག་པས་དྲི་མ་ཟད  
ཐོག་མར་བྱུང་བས་བརྒྱུད་པ་ཟད  
ཟླ་དང་བྲལ་བས་རྩིས་ལས་འདས

How wondrous!  
The dharma embodiment of great self-completeness:  
because [it] is pure from the beginning, stains are exhausted;  
because [it] arose at the beginning, transmission is exhausted;  
because [it] is without a counterpart, [it] is beyond reckoning.[^T05-029][^MTP-AUDIT-C05-221][^MTP-AUDIT-C05-222][^MTP-AUDIT-C05-223][^MTP-AUDIT-C05-287][^T05-L009][^T05-L019]

<a id="mtp-000263"></a>
<!-- pair: MTP-000263; format: verse; role: main_text -->

སྡུག་བསྔལ་རྒྱུ་མེད་བདེ་ཆེན་པོ  
བརྗོད་པའི་རྒྱུ་མེད་སྨྲ་བསམ་འདས  
ཡེ་ནས་རང་རིག་རྣམ་དག་རིག་པའོ  
ཟག་པ་ཟད་ཕྱིར་རྡོས་བཅས་མིན  
དངོས་པོ་བཟོད་ཕྱིར་སྟོང་པར་གནས

Without a cause of suffering—great bliss;  
without a cause of expression—beyond speech and thinking.  
Primordial self-awareness is completely pure awareness.  
Because outflows are exhausted, [it] is not materially encumbered;  
because [retained: དངོས་པོ་བཟོད], [it] abides empty.[^T05-029][^MTP-AUDIT-C05-224][^MTP-AUDIT-C05-225][^MTP-AUDIT-C05-226][^MTP-AUDIT-C05-227][^MTP-AUDIT-C05-287][^T05-L020][^T05-L021]

<a id="mtp-000264"></a>
<!-- pair: MTP-000264; format: verse; role: main_text -->

གཉིས་ཀྱིས་འཕེལ་མེད་ངོ་བོ་ཉིད  
ཆོས་དང་བྲལ་བས་མཐའ་ལས་འདས  
རྟོག་མེད་གསལ་བས་འགྱུ་བ་སངས  
བེམ་རིག་གཉིས་མེད་ཚོགས་གཉིས་རྫོགས

The essence itself, not increased by the two,  
is beyond extremes because [it] is free from phenomena.  
Clear and free from conceptualization, movement clears;  
without the two, matter and awareness, the two accumulations are complete.[^T05-030][^MTP-AUDIT-C05-228][^MTP-AUDIT-C05-229][^MTP-AUDIT-C05-230][^MTP-AUDIT-C05-231][^MTP-AUDIT-C05-232][^MTP-AUDIT-C05-287][^MTP-AUDIT-C05-290][^T05-L015]

<a id="mtp-000265"></a>
<!-- pair: MTP-000265; format: prose; role: source_metadata -->

> [source_metadata] ༤༦༩

> [source_metadata] [Printed page 469.][^MTP-AUDIT-C05-289][^MTP-AUDIT-C05-291]

<a id="mtp-000266"></a>
<!-- pair: MTP-000266; format: verse; role: main_text -->

འགྲན་པའི་ཟླ་མེད་ཅོག་བཞག་རང  
དེ་ཚིག་གིས་བསྒྱུར་མི་བཏུབ་པས  
བཅོས་པའི་ཚིག་མེད་ཀུན་ཏུ་ཁྱབ  
བསམ་བྱེད་སེམས་མེད་དྲན་པ་བྲལ

Without a rival counterpart—leaving as it is itself.  
Because that cannot be transformed through phrases,  
there are no contrived phrases; [it] pervades all.  
Without ordinary mind that thinks, [it] is free from mindfulness.[^T05-031][^MTP-AUDIT-C05-233][^MTP-AUDIT-C05-234][^MTP-AUDIT-C05-235][^MTP-AUDIT-C05-290]

<a id="mtp-000267"></a>
<!-- pair: MTP-000267; format: verse; role: main_text -->

སོ་མ་ཉིད་ཡིན་གཉུག་མའི་རང  
མ་བཅོས་མཆོག་ཡིན་རྣལ་མར་བཞུགས  
ཤེས་བྱ་ཉིད་ལས་བབ་ཀྱིས་གྲུབ  
སྔོན་མེད་ཕྱིས་མེད་གདོད་ནས་མེད

It is freshness itself—[retained: གཉུག་མའི་རང];  
supremely uncontrived, [it] abides authentically.  
With regard to what is to be known, [it] is established just as it settles;  
not before, not later, not from the beginning.[^T05-031][^MTP-AUDIT-C05-236][^MTP-AUDIT-C05-237][^MTP-AUDIT-C05-238][^MTP-AUDIT-C05-239][^MTP-AUDIT-C05-290][^T05-L024]

<a id="mtp-000268"></a>
<!-- pair: MTP-000268; format: verse; role: main_text -->

འགྱུ་བ་མ་ཡིན་དྲན་པ་གནས  
ཡོངས་ལ་ཁྱབ་པའི་སེམས་གཅིག་པོ  
གྲུབ་པའི་མཐའ་རྫོགས་སྤྱི་ཕུད་ཡིན  
ཐོག་མ་བར་དང་ཐ་མར་གྲུབ

[It] is not movement; mindfulness abides.  
The single ordinary mind that pervades all—  
philosophical limits complete—is the foremost portion;  
[it] is established at the beginning, middle, and end.[^T05-031][^MTP-AUDIT-C05-240][^MTP-AUDIT-C05-241][^MTP-AUDIT-C05-242][^MTP-AUDIT-C05-243][^MTP-AUDIT-C05-290][^MTP-AUDIT-C05-298][^T05-L040]

<a id="mtp-000269"></a>
<!-- pair: MTP-000269; format: verse; role: main_text -->

དེ་ལྟར་གནས་པའི་འཁྲུལ་མེད་གཞི  
རང་རིག་ཡེ་ནས་འོད་རེགས་ལ  
སྐུ་གསུམ་ཡེ་ནས་མ་བསྐྱེད་རྫོགས

The undeluded Ground abiding in this way—  
self-awareness, primordially [retained: འོད་རེགས་ལ]—  
the three embodiments are primordially complete, without being generated.[^T05-032][^MTP-AUDIT-C05-244][^MTP-AUDIT-C05-245][^MTP-AUDIT-C05-290][^MTP-AUDIT-C05-293]

<a id="mtp-000270"></a>
<!-- pair: MTP-000270; format: prose; role: source_metadata -->

> [source_metadata] ༤༧༠

> [source_metadata] [Printed page 470.][^MTP-AUDIT-C05-292][^MTP-AUDIT-C05-294]

<a id="mtp-000271"></a>
<!-- pair: MTP-000271; format: verse; role: main_text -->

འབྲས་བུ་ཡེ་ནས་རང་གནས་པ  
ཆོས་ཀྱིས་ཡེ་ནས་བཅོས་སུ་མེད  
གྲུབ་མཐས་ཡེ་ནས་བསྐྱངས་དང་བྲལ  
དུག་ལྔས་ཡེ་ནས་གོས་པ་མེད  
འཁོར་བ་འདས་འདུ་འབྲལ་ག་ལ་ཡོད

The result abides of itself primordially;  
primordially, it cannot be contrived through phenomena.  
Primordially, it is free from being sustained by tenet systems;  
primordially, it is unstained by the five poisons.  
Where could there be gathering or separation of cyclic existence and transcendence of sorrow?[^T05-033][^CH05-G000902][^MTP-AUDIT-C05-246][^MTP-AUDIT-C05-247][^MTP-AUDIT-C05-248][^MTP-AUDIT-C05-249][^MTP-AUDIT-C05-250][^MTP-AUDIT-C05-293][^T05-L027]

<a id="mtp-000272"></a>
<!-- pair: MTP-000272; format: verse; role: main_text -->

གསལ་བའི་ངོས་ནི་བརྗིད་རེ་ཆེ  
སྣང་བ་ཉིད་ནི་ལྷུན་རེ་སྡུག  
ཆོས་སྐུ་ངོ་སྤྲོད་དང་རེ་སྤྲོ  
ངོ་བོ་ཟང་ཐལ་སྦུབས་རེ་ཡངས

The face of clarity—how great its majesty!  
Appearance itself—how spontaneously beautiful!  
Pointing out the dharma embodiment—how lucidly joyful!  
The essence, unimpeded penetration—how spacious its interior![^T05-034][^MTP-AUDIT-C05-251][^MTP-AUDIT-C05-252][^MTP-AUDIT-C05-253][^MTP-AUDIT-C05-254][^MTP-AUDIT-C05-293]

<a id="mtp-000273"></a>
<!-- pair: MTP-000273; format: verse; role: main_text -->

རང་བཞིན་ཀུན་ཁྱབ་གདལ་རེ་ཆེ  
ཐུགས་རྗེ་ཀུན་འབྱུང་སྐྱོ་རེ་ཆེ  
ཁྱབ་པ་ཡེ་གདངས་བཀྲག་རེ་གསལ  
གནས་པ་ཐོགས་མེད་བང་རེ་མགྱོགས  
རིག་པའི་རང་གདངས་འོད་རེ་གསལ

Intrinsic nature, all-pervading—how great its spread!  
Compassionate responsiveness, source of all—how great [retained: སྐྱོ]!  
Pervasion, primordial radiance—how clear its brilliance!  
Abiding, unobstructed—how swift its pace!  
Awareness’s own radiance—how clear its light![^T05-034][^MTP-AUDIT-C05-255][^MTP-AUDIT-C05-256][^MTP-AUDIT-C05-257][^MTP-AUDIT-C05-258][^MTP-AUDIT-C05-259][^MTP-AUDIT-C05-293][^T05-L031]

<a id="mtp-000274"></a>
<!-- pair: MTP-000274; format: prose; role: source_metadata -->

> [source_metadata] ༤༧༡

> [source_metadata] [Printed page 471.][^MTP-AUDIT-C05-295][^MTP-AUDIT-C05-297]

<a id="mtp-000275"></a>
<!-- pair: MTP-000275; format: verse; role: main_text -->

སྟོང་པ་ཀུན་འབྱུང་ངོ་རེ་མཚར  
མ་བཙལ་བཞག་པ་རྒྱུན་རེ་རིང  
མ་བཀོད་རང་བྱུང་བརྡལ་རེ་ཆེ  
སྣང་སྲིད་གཞིར་བཞེངས་དཔངས་རིམ་ཐོ

Emptiness, source of all—how wondrous!  
Leaving [it] without seeking—how long its continuity!  
Unarranged, naturally arising—how great its spread!  
Appearance and existence arise as the Ground—its height rises by stages.[^T05-035][^MTP-AUDIT-C05-260][^MTP-AUDIT-C05-261][^MTP-AUDIT-C05-262][^MTP-AUDIT-C05-296]

<a id="mtp-000276"></a>
<!-- pair: MTP-000276; format: verse; role: main_text -->

འབད་མེད་རང་གྲོལ་རྒྱ་རེ་ཆེ  
ཡེ་རྟོགས་ཆེན་པོ་འཁོར་རེ་མཁས  
ཁ་དོག་ལྔ་ལྡན་བྲི་རེ་ལེགས  
སྣང་སྲིད་རྣམ་དག་ཁོང་རེ་སྙོམས

Effortless self-liberation—how vast its extent!  
Great primordial realization—how skilled its turning!  
Endowed with five colors—how well painted!  
Completely pure appearance and existence—how even within![^T05-035][^MTP-AUDIT-C05-263][^MTP-AUDIT-C05-264][^MTP-AUDIT-C05-296][^T05-L031]

<a id="mtp-000277"></a>
<!-- pair: MTP-000277; format: verse; role: main_text -->

རང་བྱུང་འོད་གསལ་བཀོད་རེ་ལེགས  
གཞི་སྣང་ཆེན་པོ་ཤོངས་རེ་ཆེ  
འཁོར་འདས་གཉིས་པོ་འབྲེལ་རེ་མཁས  
སྐུ་ལྔ་ཡེ་ཤེས་བརྩེགས་རེ་ལེགས

Naturally arising clear light—how well arranged!  
Great Ground-appearance—how vast its capacity!  
The two, cyclic existence and transcendence of sorrow—how skillfully connected!  
Five embodiments and primordial knowing—how well arrayed in layers![^T05-035][^MTP-AUDIT-C05-265][^MTP-AUDIT-C05-266][^MTP-AUDIT-C05-267][^MTP-AUDIT-C05-268][^MTP-AUDIT-C05-296][^T05-L016]

<a id="mtp-000278"></a>
<!-- pair: MTP-000278; format: verse; role: main_text -->

རང་བཞིན་གནས་པའི་ངོ་བོ་ལ  
སངས་རྒྱས་མེད་ཅིང་སེམས་ཅན་མེད  
མ་རིག་མེད་ཅིང་འཁྲུལ་པ་མེད

In the essence abiding as intrinsic nature,  
there are no buddhas and no karmic beings;  
there is no ignorance and no delusion.[^T05-036][^MTP-AUDIT-C05-269][^MTP-AUDIT-C05-270][^MTP-AUDIT-C05-296][^MTP-AUDIT-C05-299][^T05-L025]

<a id="mtp-000279"></a>
<!-- pair: MTP-000279; format: prose; role: source_metadata -->

> [source_metadata] ༤༧༢

> [source_metadata] [Printed page 472.][^MTP-AUDIT-C05-398][^MTP-AUDIT-C05-399]

<a id="mtp-000280"></a>
<!-- pair: MTP-000280; format: verse; role: main_text -->

སེམས་མེད་ཡིད་ཀྱང་ཡོད་མ་ཡིན  
དགྲ་མེད་བཤེས་མེད་གཉེན་ཡང་མེད  
ཉོན་མོངས་མེད་ཅིང་ངར་འཛིན་མེད  
འགྲོ་བ་མེད་ལ་འོང་བ་མེད

There is no ordinary mind, nor is there mental faculty;  
there are no enemies, friends, or kin.  
There are no afflictions and no grasping at “I”;  
there is no going and no coming.[^T05-036][^MTP-AUDIT-C05-300][^MTP-AUDIT-C05-301][^MTP-AUDIT-C05-302][^MTP-AUDIT-C05-303][^MTP-AUDIT-C05-398][^MTP-AUDIT-C05-399][^T05-L026]

<a id="mtp-000281"></a>
<!-- pair: MTP-000281; format: verse; role: main_text -->

ཆོས་མེད་ཆོས་སུ་སྣང་བ་མེད  
ཐབས་མེད་ཤེས་རབ་ཉིད་ཀྱང་མེད  
ཕུང་པོ་མེད་ཅིང་ཁམས་ཀྱང་མེད  
ཡུལ་མེད་འཛིན་པར་བྱ་བ་མེད

There are no phenomena and no appearing as phenomena;  
there are no means, nor discerning knowing itself.  
There are no aggregates, nor elements;  
there are no objects, nothing to be apprehended.[^T05-036][^MTP-AUDIT-C05-304][^MTP-AUDIT-C05-305][^MTP-AUDIT-C05-306][^MTP-AUDIT-C05-307][^MTP-AUDIT-C05-398][^MTP-AUDIT-C05-399][^T05-L027][^T05-L028][^T05-L029]

<a id="mtp-000282"></a>
<!-- pair: MTP-000282; format: verse; role: main_text -->

དབང་པོ་མེད་ཅིང་ཡུལ་ཡང་མེད  
གཟུང་བར་བྱ་ཞིང་འཛིན་པ་མེད  
སྐུ་མེད་ཡེ་ཤེས་ཉིད་ཀྱང་མེད  
ཤེས་དང་ཤེས་བྱའི་བདག་པོ་མེད

There are no faculties, nor objects;  
there is what is to be apprehended, and no apprehending subject.  
There are no embodiments, nor primordial knowing itself;  
there is no lord of knowing and what is to be known.[^T05-036][^MTP-AUDIT-C05-308][^MTP-AUDIT-C05-309][^MTP-AUDIT-C05-310][^MTP-AUDIT-C05-398][^MTP-AUDIT-C05-399][^T05-L029][^T05-L030]

<a id="mtp-000283"></a>
<!-- pair: MTP-000283; format: verse; role: main_text -->

འོད་མེད་ཁ་དོག་ཡོད་མ་ཡིན  
སྒྲ་མེད་དྲི་ལ་སོགས་པ་མེད  
འདོད་པ་མེད་ལ་ཞེན་པ་མེད  
དབྱིངས་མེད་གཞལ་ཡས་ཁང་ཡང་མེད  
ལྷ་མེད་མཆོད་པའི་ཡུལ་འདས་པའོ

There is no light, nor color;  
there is no sound, no smell or the like.  
There is no desire and no clinging;  
there is no basic space, nor a measureless palace.  
There is no deity; [it] is beyond the object of offering.[^T05-037][^CH05-G000944][^MTP-AUDIT-C05-311][^MTP-AUDIT-C05-312][^MTP-AUDIT-C05-313][^MTP-AUDIT-C05-314][^MTP-AUDIT-C05-398][^MTP-AUDIT-C05-399][^MTP-AUDIT-C05-400][^MTP-AUDIT-C05-401][^T05-L031]

<a id="mtp-000284"></a>
<!-- pair: MTP-000284; format: prose; role: source_metadata -->

> [source_metadata] ༤༧༣

> [source_metadata] [Printed page 473.][^MTP-AUDIT-C05-400][^MTP-AUDIT-C05-401]

<a id="mtp-000285"></a>
<!-- pair: MTP-000285; format: verse; role: main_text -->

ཀྱེ་མ་གསང་བདག་རྡོ་རྗེ་འཛིན  
ཁྱད་པར་ཆེན་པོའི་སྣང་བ་ལ  
ཡོད་དང་མེད་པ་གཉིས་བྱུང་སྟེ  
སྤྱིས་གཞི་འཁྲུལ་གཞི་ཞེས་བྱ་བ

O Lord of Secrets, holder of the vajra!  
In the appearance of the great distinction,  
existence and nonexistence, the two, arise—  
what is called “[retained: སྤྱིས་གཞི], Ground of delusion.”[^T05-038][^MTP-AUDIT-C05-315][^MTP-AUDIT-C05-316][^MTP-AUDIT-C05-317][^MTP-AUDIT-C05-400][^MTP-AUDIT-C05-401][^T05-L042]

<a id="mtp-000286"></a>
<!-- pair: MTP-000286; format: verse; role: main_text -->

མ་རིག་ཉིད་དང་སྦགས་པའི་ཕྱིར  
ཤེས་བྱ་ཉིད་ཀྱང་དྲི་མར་སྣང  
སེམས་ལས་དྲན་བསམ་འཆར་བའི་ཕྱིར  
ངོ་བོ་ཉིད་ནི་རྟོག་པས་སྔགས

Because [it] is mixed with ignorance itself,  
even what is to be known appears as stain.  
Because mindfulness and thinking arise from ordinary mind,  
the essence itself, through conceptual thought—[retained: སྔགས].[^T05-038][^MTP-AUDIT-C05-318][^MTP-AUDIT-C05-319][^MTP-AUDIT-C05-320][^MTP-AUDIT-C05-400][^MTP-AUDIT-C05-401][^T05-L030][^T05-L032]

<a id="mtp-000287"></a>
<!-- pair: MTP-000287; format: verse; role: main_text -->

ཡིད་དྲུག་འཛིན་པ་མ་འགགས་ཕྱིར  
ཆོས་སྐུ་ཉིད་ཀྱང་བཟུང་བས་བཅིངས  
ཆ་མེད་རྡུལ་དང་བཅས་པའི་ཕྱིར  
འོད་གསལ་ཉིད་ཀྱང་བག་ལ་ཆགས

Because the six mental faculties’ apprehending is unceasing,  
even the dharma embodiment itself is bound by apprehending.  
Because [it] is accompanied by partless particles,  
even clear light itself settles into latency.[^T05-039][^MTP-AUDIT-C05-321][^MTP-AUDIT-C05-322][^MTP-AUDIT-C05-323][^MTP-AUDIT-C05-324][^MTP-AUDIT-C05-400][^MTP-AUDIT-C05-401]

<a id="mtp-000288"></a>
<!-- pair: MTP-000288; format: verse; role: main_text -->

རྐྱེན་བཞི་དམིགས་པ་མ་དག་ཕྱིར  
སྣང་བ་ཉིད་ནི་དུ་མར་ཤར

Because the objects of focus of the four conditions are impure,  
appearance itself arises as many.[^T05-039][^MTP-AUDIT-C05-325][^MTP-AUDIT-C05-326][^MTP-AUDIT-C05-400][^MTP-AUDIT-C05-401]

<a id="mtp-000289"></a>
<!-- pair: MTP-000289; format: prose; role: source_metadata -->

> [source_metadata] ༤༧༤

> [source_metadata] [Printed page 474.][^MTP-AUDIT-C05-402][^MTP-AUDIT-C05-403]

<a id="mtp-000290"></a>
<!-- pair: MTP-000290; format: verse; role: main_text -->

སྣང་བ་དེ་ཉིད་ཡུལ་སྣང་ཕྱིར  
མཁའ་ལ་རླུང་བརྟེན་དེ་ལ་ཆུ  
འབུམ་ཕྲག་བཅུ་གཅིག་དག་གིས་ནི  
ཟླུམ་ལ་འདྲིལ་བ་ཉིད་དུ་ཆགས

Because that very appearance appears as objects,  
wind rests on space, and on that, water;  
with eleven hundred-thousands,  
[it] forms into a rolled sphere.[^T05-040][^MTP-AUDIT-C05-327][^MTP-AUDIT-C05-328][^MTP-AUDIT-C05-329][^MTP-AUDIT-C05-402][^MTP-AUDIT-C05-403][^T05-L033]

<a id="mtp-000291"></a>
<!-- pair: MTP-000291; format: verse; role: main_text -->

འོ་མ་ལས་ནི་སྤྲིས་འབྱུང་བཞིན  
ས་ནི་འབུམ་ཕྲག་བཅུ་གཉིས་སོ  
རླུང་གི་གྲངས་ནི་ས་ཡ་ཉིད

As a film forms on milk,  
earth is twelve hundred-thousands;  
the number for wind is a million.[^T05-040][^MTP-AUDIT-C05-330][^MTP-AUDIT-C05-331][^MTP-AUDIT-C05-332][^MTP-AUDIT-C05-402][^MTP-AUDIT-C05-403]

<a id="mtp-000292"></a>
<!-- pair: MTP-000292; format: verse; role: main_text -->

རླུང་ནི་ཆགས་དང་སྡུད་པ་དང  
འཕེན་པ་དང་ནི་འདེགས་པའོ  
ཆུ་ནི་ཁྱབ་དང་འཛིན་པ་དང  
སྨིན་བྱེད་དང་ནི་གསུམ་ཡིན་ནོ

Wind forms, gathers,  
propels, and lifts.  
Water pervades, holds,  
and ripens: these are three.[^T05-041][^MTP-AUDIT-C05-333][^MTP-AUDIT-C05-334][^MTP-AUDIT-C05-402][^MTP-AUDIT-C05-403]

<a id="mtp-000293"></a>
<!-- pair: MTP-000293; format: verse; role: main_text -->

ས་ནི་སྒྲུབ་བྱེད་ཕྲ་བ་དང  
རགས་པ་ཉིད་དང་གསུམ་ཡིན་ནོ

Earth accomplishes, is subtle,  
and is coarse: these are three.[^T05-041][^MTP-AUDIT-C05-335][^MTP-AUDIT-C05-336][^MTP-AUDIT-C05-402][^MTP-AUDIT-C05-403]

<a id="mtp-000294"></a>
<!-- pair: MTP-000294; format: verse; role: main_text -->

དཀྲོགས་ནས་མར་དུ་འགྱུར་བ་བཞིན  
རི་རབ་རི་ཡི་རྒྱལ་པོ་ནི  
བང་རིམ་བཞི་དང་ལྕོག་ཆེན་དང  
རོལ་མཚོ་དང་ནི་རི་རྣམས་བདུན

As churning turns [milk] into butter,  
Mount Meru, king of mountains,  
with four terraces and a great turret,  
the encircling seas and seven mountains,[^T05-042][^MTP-AUDIT-C05-337][^MTP-AUDIT-C05-338][^MTP-AUDIT-C05-339][^MTP-AUDIT-C05-340][^MTP-AUDIT-C05-402][^MTP-AUDIT-C05-403]

<a id="mtp-000295"></a>
<!-- pair: MTP-000295; format: prose; role: source_metadata -->

> [source_metadata] ༤༧༥

> [source_metadata] [Printed page 475.][^MTP-AUDIT-C05-404][^MTP-AUDIT-C05-405]

<a id="mtp-000296"></a>
<!-- pair: MTP-000296; format: verse; role: main_text -->

གླིང་རྣམས་དང་ནི་ཁོར་ཡུག་ཆགས  
སྟོང་གསུམ་ལ་སོགས་ཁམས་རྣམས་སོ  
འདོད་དང་གཟུགས་དང་གཟུགས་མེད་ཀྱི  
ཁམས་གསུམ་པོ་ནི་དེ་ལ་བརྟེན

the continents and the outer rim take shape.  
These are realms such as the three-thousand [world-system].  
Desire, form, and formlessness—  
the three realms—depend on that.[^T05-042][^MTP-AUDIT-C05-341][^MTP-AUDIT-C05-342][^MTP-AUDIT-C05-343][^MTP-AUDIT-C05-404][^MTP-AUDIT-C05-405]

<a id="mtp-000297"></a>
<!-- pair: MTP-000297; format: verse; role: main_text -->

ཡོད་མིན་ཅི་ཡང་མེད་པའི་དང  
རྣམ་ཤེས་ཅུང་ཟད་མེད་པ་ཡི  
གཟུགས་མེད་རྣམས་ནི་དེ་ལས་སྐྱེས

Not existing, and nothing at all,  
and not the slightest consciousness—  
the formless ones arise from that.[^T05-043][^MTP-AUDIT-C05-344][^MTP-AUDIT-C05-345][^MTP-AUDIT-C05-346][^MTP-AUDIT-C05-404][^MTP-AUDIT-C05-405][^T05-L041]

<a id="mtp-000298"></a>
<!-- pair: MTP-000298; format: verse; role: main_text -->

གནས་གཙང་ལ་སོགས་ལུས་ཅན་ནི  
གོང་ནས་འོག་ཏུ་ཐེགས་པའི་ཚུལ  
འོག་མིན་ལ་སོགས་འདོད་པའོ

The embodied ones of the Pure Abodes and the like,  
in the manner of descending from above to below—  
Akaniṣṭha and the like, desire.[^T05-043][^MTP-AUDIT-C05-347][^MTP-AUDIT-C05-348][^MTP-AUDIT-C05-349][^MTP-AUDIT-C05-404][^MTP-AUDIT-C05-405]

<a id="mtp-000299"></a>
<!-- pair: MTP-000299; format: verse; role: main_text -->

ལྷ་དང་ལྷ་མིན་རྣམས་ལ་ནི  
མངོན་མཐོའོ་དེ་ནས་གནས་གསུམ་ནི  
བྱོལ་སོང་ཡི་དགས་དམྱལ་བ་དང

For gods and demigods,  
[there is] higher existence; then the three places—  
animals, hungry ghosts, and hell beings;[^T05-043][^CH05-G000994][^MTP-AUDIT-C05-350][^MTP-AUDIT-C05-351][^MTP-AUDIT-C05-352][^MTP-AUDIT-C05-404][^MTP-AUDIT-C05-405][^MTP-AUDIT-C05-406][^MTP-AUDIT-C05-407][^T05-L025]

<a id="mtp-000300"></a>
<!-- pair: MTP-000300; format: prose; role: source_metadata -->

> [source_metadata] ༤༧༦

> [source_metadata] [Printed page 476.][^MTP-AUDIT-C05-406][^MTP-AUDIT-C05-407]

<a id="mtp-000301"></a>
<!-- pair: MTP-000301; format: verse; role: main_text -->

སེམས་ཅན་དེ་དག་སྐྱེ་བ་བཞི

those karmic beings [have] four births.[^T05-043][^MTP-AUDIT-C05-353][^MTP-AUDIT-C05-406][^MTP-AUDIT-C05-407][^T05-L025]

<a id="mtp-000302"></a>
<!-- pair: MTP-000302; format: verse; role: main_text -->

དེ་ཡང་བསྐལ་པ་དང་པོའི་མི  
གཟུགས་དང་ལང་ཚོ་དཔེ་བྱད་དང  
རབ་འོད་མདངས་ཀྱིས་ལུས་ཁྱབ་སྟེ  
འདོད་འཇོ་ལ་སོགས་བསོད་ནམས་ཟས  
སྐྱེད་གུའི་ཚེ་ཡང་བརྒྱད་ཁྲི་ལ  
ཕོ་དང་མོ་ཡི་དབང་པོ་མེད

Furthermore, the humans of the first aeon,  
with form, youth, and bodily signs,  
their bodies pervaded by intense light and luster,  
[had] food of merit, such as wish-yielding [food];  
the lifespan of creatures, too, was eighty thousand,  
and there were no male or female organs.[^T05-044][^MTP-AUDIT-C05-354][^MTP-AUDIT-C05-355][^MTP-AUDIT-C05-356][^MTP-AUDIT-C05-357][^MTP-AUDIT-C05-358][^MTP-AUDIT-C05-406][^MTP-AUDIT-C05-407][^T05-L025][^T05-L031][^T05-L005]

<a id="mtp-000303"></a>
<!-- pair: MTP-000303; format: verse; role: main_text -->

དེ་ནས་འཁྲུལ་པའི་རྒྱུ་གཉིས་ལས  
འདོད་ཆགས་ལ་སོགས་ཉོན་མོངས་ནི  
བགས་ཀྱིས་རགས་པ་ཉིད་དུ་གྱུར  
དབང་པོ་ལ་སོགས་སྒོ་དོད་དེ  
བསོད་ནམས་ཟད་ཕྱིར་འོད་སོགས་བྲི

Then, from the two causes of delusion,  
afflictions such as desire  
gradually became coarse.  
Gates, such as the faculties, emerged;  
because merit was exhausted, light and the like diminished.[^T05-044][^MTP-AUDIT-C05-359][^MTP-AUDIT-C05-360][^MTP-AUDIT-C05-361][^MTP-AUDIT-C05-362][^MTP-AUDIT-C05-406][^MTP-AUDIT-C05-407][^T05-L026][^T05-L029][^T05-L031][^T05-L034]

<a id="mtp-000304"></a>
<!-- pair: MTP-000304; format: verse; role: main_text -->

དེ་ནས་རིམ་གྱིས་ས་ཚིལ་དང

Then, gradually, earth-fat[^T05-044][^MTP-AUDIT-C05-363][^MTP-AUDIT-C05-406][^MTP-AUDIT-C05-407][^MTP-AUDIT-C05-408][^MTP-AUDIT-C05-409]

<a id="mtp-000305"></a>
<!-- pair: MTP-000305; format: prose; role: source_metadata -->

> [source_metadata] ༤༧༧

> [source_metadata] [Printed page 477.][^MTP-AUDIT-C05-408][^MTP-AUDIT-C05-409]

<a id="mtp-000306"></a>
<!-- pair: MTP-000306; format: verse; role: main_text -->

ས་ལུ་ལ་སོགས་ཟས་སྤྱོད་དོ

and rice and the like were consumed as food.[^T05-044][^MTP-AUDIT-C05-364][^MTP-AUDIT-C05-408][^MTP-AUDIT-C05-409]

<a id="mtp-000307"></a>
<!-- pair: MTP-000307; format: verse; role: main_text -->

དེ་ལྟར་འཇིག་རྟེན་གསུམ་པ་ཉིད  
ཕུང་པོ་ལྔ་དང་དབང་པོ་ལྔ  
ཡན་ལག་ལྔ་དང་དོན་སྙིང་ལྔ  
ཡུལ་ལྔ་དང་ནི་ཉོན་མོངས་ལྔ  
སེམས་ལྔ་ཡིད་ལྔ་རྟོག་པ་ལྔ  
གཟུང་འཛིན་འཁོར་བ་ཉིད་དུ་གྲུབ

Thus, the three worlds themselves—  
five aggregates and five faculties,  
five limbs and five vital organs,  
five objects and five afflictions,  
five ordinary minds, five mental faculties, five conceptual thoughts—  
are established as cyclic existence of apprehended object and apprehending subject.[^T05-045][^MTP-AUDIT-C05-365][^MTP-AUDIT-C05-366][^MTP-AUDIT-C05-367][^MTP-AUDIT-C05-368][^MTP-AUDIT-C05-369][^MTP-AUDIT-C05-370][^MTP-AUDIT-C05-408][^MTP-AUDIT-C05-409][^T05-L026][^T05-L029][^T05-L035]

<a id="mtp-000308"></a>
<!-- pair: MTP-000308; format: verse; role: main_text -->

འཁྲུལ་པ་གཉིས་སྣང་དེ་ལྟ་བུ  
དཔེར་ན་མྱུར་འགྲོའི་འཁོར་ལོ་བཞིན  
རྒྱུད་ཆགས་འཁོར་ལ་ཡུན་རིང་གནས

Delusion’s twofold appearance is like that.  
For example, like a swiftly moving wheel,  
[it] revolves in a connected continuum and abides for a long time.[^T05-045][^MTP-AUDIT-C05-371][^MTP-AUDIT-C05-372][^MTP-AUDIT-C05-373][^MTP-AUDIT-C05-408][^MTP-AUDIT-C05-409]

<a id="mtp-000309"></a>
<!-- pair: MTP-000309; format: verse; role: main_text -->

དེ་ལྟར་སྣང་བ་སྣ་ཚོགས་རྣམས  
ཐག་པ་ལས་ནི་སྤྲུལ་མཐོང་བཞིན

The various appearances in this way  
are like seeing [retained: སྤྲུལ] from a rope:[^T05-046][^MTP-AUDIT-C05-374][^MTP-AUDIT-C05-408][^MTP-AUDIT-C05-409]

<a id="mtp-000310"></a>
<!-- pair: MTP-000310; format: prose; role: source_metadata -->

> [source_metadata] ༤༧༨

> [source_metadata] [Printed page 478.][^MTP-AUDIT-C05-410][^MTP-AUDIT-C05-411]

<a id="mtp-000311"></a>
<!-- pair: MTP-000311; format: verse; role: main_text -->

དེ་མ་ཡིན་ལ་དེར་བཟུང་བས  
ཕྱི་ནང་སྣོད་བཅུད་གཉིས་སུ་ཆགས  
དེ་ཡང་བརྟགས་ན་ཐག་པ་ཉིད  
སྣོད་བཅུད་ཡེ་ནས་སྟོང་པ་སྟེ  
དོན་དམ་ཀུན་རྫོབ་གཟུགས་ཅན་ནོ

by apprehending as that what is not that,  
outer and inner, the environment and its inhabitants, form as two.  
Yet, when examined, [it] is the rope itself;  
the environment and its inhabitants are primordially empty.  
Ultimate and conventional—endowed with form.[^T05-046][^CH05-G001027][^MTP-AUDIT-C05-375][^MTP-AUDIT-C05-376][^MTP-AUDIT-C05-377][^MTP-AUDIT-C05-378][^MTP-AUDIT-C05-379][^MTP-AUDIT-C05-410][^MTP-AUDIT-C05-411]

<a id="mtp-000312"></a>
<!-- pair: MTP-000312; format: verse; role: main_text -->

སྤྲུལ་མཐོང་དེ་ཉིད་མཐོང་བས་བདེན  
ཐག་པ་ཡང་དག་ཉིད་དུ་བདེན  
དཔེར་ན་མཐོ་ལ་བྱ་གནས་བཞིན

That seeing of [retained: སྤྲུལ] is true through being seen;  
the rope is true as authentic [reality].  
For example, like a bird abiding on a high place,[^T05-046][^MTP-AUDIT-C05-380][^MTP-AUDIT-C05-381][^MTP-AUDIT-C05-382][^MTP-AUDIT-C05-410][^MTP-AUDIT-C05-411]

<a id="mtp-000313"></a>
<!-- pair: MTP-000313; format: verse; role: main_text -->

བདེན་པ་གཉིས་ཀྱི་རང་བཞིན་ནི  
འཇིག་རྟེན་ཀུན་རྫོབ་ཙམ་དུ་སྟེ  
ཡང་དག་ཉིད་འབྲེལ་པ་མེད

the intrinsic nature of the two truths  
is merely worldly convention,  
without relation to authenticity itself.[^T05-047][^CH05-G001032][^MTP-AUDIT-C05-383][^MTP-AUDIT-C05-384][^MTP-AUDIT-C05-397][^MTP-AUDIT-C05-410][^MTP-AUDIT-C05-411]

<a id="mtp-000314"></a>
<!-- pair: MTP-000314; format: verse; role: main_text -->

སྟོང་པ་ཉིད་ཀྱི་དབྱིངས་ལས་ནི  
དེ་ཡི་ངོ་བོར་ཐམས་ཅད་གྲོལ  
རིག་པ་སྟོང་གསལ་ཁྱབ་ཆེ་བས  
བཅུད་རྣམས་སྙིང་པོ་ཉིད་དུ་དྲིལ

From the basic space of emptiness,  
all is liberated as its essence.  
Since awareness, empty and clear, pervades greatly,  
the inhabitants are gathered into the core itself.[^T05-047][^MTP-AUDIT-C05-385][^MTP-AUDIT-C05-386][^MTP-AUDIT-C05-387][^MTP-AUDIT-C05-388][^MTP-AUDIT-C05-410][^MTP-AUDIT-C05-411][^MTP-AUDIT-C05-412][^MTP-AUDIT-C05-413][^T05-L036][^T05-L037]

<a id="mtp-000316"></a>
<!-- pair: MTP-000316; format: verse; role: main_text -->

དེ་ལ་སྙིང་པོ་དེ་ཉིད་ལ  
སྐུ་ལྔ་ཡེ་ཤེས་ལྔ་དང་ནི  
འོད་ལྔ་ཁ་དོག་རྣམ་པ་ལྔ  
ཆོས་ལྔ་ཡེ་ཤེས་རླུང་ལྔ་ཉིད  
ངོ་བོ་ལྔ་དང་རང་བཞིན་ལྔ  
ཐུགས་རྗེ་ལྔ་ཡིས་ཀུན་ལ་ཁྱབ

There, in that very core,  
the five embodiments and five primordial knowings,  
five lights, five aspects of color,  
five phenomena, five winds of primordial knowing,  
five essences and five intrinsic natures—  
with five compassionate responsivenesses, [it] pervades all.[^T05-048][^MTP-AUDIT-C05-389][^MTP-AUDIT-C05-390][^MTP-AUDIT-C05-391][^MTP-AUDIT-C05-392][^MTP-AUDIT-C05-393][^MTP-AUDIT-C05-412][^MTP-AUDIT-C05-413][^T05-L027][^T05-L031][^T05-L036]

<a id="mtp-000317"></a>
<!-- pair: MTP-000317; format: verse; role: main_text -->

དྲན་མེད་འགྱུ་བ་ཉིད་ཀྱང་མེད  
ཆོས་ཀྱི་དབྱིངས་ཀྱི་ཆོ་འཕྲུལ་ལ  
ཐམས་ཅད་དེ་ཡི་ངང་དུ་འདུས  
གཅིག་དང་གཉིས་ནི་ཉག་གཅིག་པོ

There is no mindfulness, nor movement itself;  
in the magical display of the basic space of phenomena,  
all is gathered into its state.  
One and two are the unique [one].”[^T05-048][^MTP-AUDIT-C05-394][^MTP-AUDIT-C05-395][^MTP-AUDIT-C05-396][^MTP-AUDIT-C05-412][^MTP-AUDIT-C05-413]

## Endnotes

[^T05-001]: Golden-v1 chapter 5, S637, S638, S639, S640, S649, S650, S654, S736, S739, S766 (printed 454–461). Names and titles are provisional whole expressions, not additions to the canonical glossary: ཁྲོ་བོ / ཁྲོ་བདག are wrathful ones / lord of wrathful ones; རིགས here is families, with no named taxonomy supplied; བཅོམ་ལྡན is Bhagavān; རྡོ་རྗེ་འཆང is Vajradhara. The distinct རྡོ་རྗེ་འཛིན is holder of the vajra, not an automatic application of apprehending subject to every འཛིན compound. གསང་བ་མཆོག་བདག is provisionally Supreme Lord of Secrets. སངས་རྒྱས / སེམས་ཅན are buddhas / sentient beings; the latter is a whole expression and does not replace standalone ordinary mind. At S654, ཀུན་བདག is read as lord of all in apposition to the one mandala; exact attachment remains for review. Quotation boundaries are editorial presentation.

    **Phase D current disposition (2026-10-05; supersedes earlier terminology-status claims only):** Active row `བཅོམ་ལྡན་` → **Blessed One**. The full row’s conditions govern, not a component-only substitution. Active row `རྡོ་རྗེ་འཆང་` → **Vajradhara**. The full row’s conditions govern, not a component-only substitution. Active row `སེམས་ཅན་` → **karmic being**. The full row’s conditions govern, not a component-only substitution. PD-001, MTP-000188: `བཅོམ་ལྡན`; current English **Blessed One** replaces “Bhagavān”. PD-004, MTP-000188: `སེམས་ཅན`; current English **karmic beings** replaces “sentient beings”. PD-004, MTP-000220: `སེམས་ཅན`; current English **karmic beings** replaces “sentient beings”. See [the whole-work review](https://github.com/Lotus-King-Translation/Mu-Tig-Phreng-Ba/blob/review/phase-d-20261005/translations/FINAL-REVIEW.md#phase-d-review). Original source-audit evidence and approval history above are retained; unresolved constructions remain flagged.

[^T05-002]: Golden-v1 chapter 5, S639, S640, S641, S642, S643, S644, S645, S646, S647 (printed 454). The questions are not silently answered or normalized. S639 རང་བཞིན་གང is read interrogatively as “what is the intrinsic nature”; a relative reading, “the intrinsic nature which ...,” is also possible. S643 repeats གང twice and the English preserves that repetition without identifying two means. S645 བདག་ཅག་རྣམས་ནི་གྱུར་ན has no securely supplied complement: “If we have become [—]” remains locally unresolved. “Become that primordial knowing” would be a reconstruction from context, not overt wording. S646 གཉིས་སྣང is provisionally twofold appearance; what the two are is not supplied. Review the interrogative attachment and missing complement against the source layers.

[^T05-003]: Golden-v1 chapter 5, S650, S651, S652, S653, S654 (printed 455). S651 exact གཉིས་ནི་གཞི་ངོ་བོ་གཅིག་ཁྱད་པར་ལས is preserved as a compressed sequence: “The two—the Ground, one in essence; from distinction—”. The referent of “two,” the attachment of ཁྱད་པར་ལས, and the subject of S652 གནས remain unsettled. The English does not insert a causal origin story or remove the expanded words. S652 སྤྲོས་མེད is provisionally without conceptual elaborations, preserving the family of canonical སྤྲོས་བྲལ without claiming an exact entry. S653 བདེ་ཆེན is great bliss; S654 དཀྱིལ་འཁོར is mandala, both local proposals. The following descriptions are provisionally appositional; review their attachment. The Ground retains the canonical display capitalization pending a separate presentation decision.

    **Phase D current disposition (2026-10-05; supersedes earlier terminology-status claims only):** Active row `དཀྱིལ་འཁོར་` → **mandala**. The full row’s conditions govern, not a component-only substitution. Active row `དགོངས་པ་` → **Enlightened intent**. The full row’s conditions govern, not a component-only substitution. See [the whole-work review](https://github.com/Lotus-King-Translation/Mu-Tig-Phreng-Ba/blob/review/phase-d-20261005/translations/FINAL-REVIEW.md#phase-d-review). Original source-audit evidence and approval history above are retained; unresolved constructions remain flagged.

[^T05-004]: Golden-v1 chapter 5, S655, S656, S657, S658, S659, S660 (printed 455). S655 ངོ་བོ་ཉིད and S656 རང་བཞིན་ཉིད retain essence / intrinsic nature with the intensive itself. ཀ་དག་པས is the instrumental/causal primordial-purity construction; ལྷུན་གྲུབ remains spontaneous presence. S657 ས is locally grounds, an uncovered term, not the separate canonical The Ground for གཞི. S658 ལྟར་སྣང is provisionally seeming appearance; it is not the view entry merely because it contains ལྟ. S660 བབས་ཀྱིས་གྲུབ is tentatively established just as it settles; the subject is unexpressed and the precise force of བབས remains for review. No separate “spontaneous” term is imported.

[^T05-005]: Golden-v1 chapter 5, S662, S663, S664, S665, S666, S667, S668, S669 (printed 456). At S662 ལུང is provisionally scriptural transmission and རིགས་པ is reasoning, distinct from awareness རིག་པ. S663 རྒྱུད is provisionally tantra in this textual list, rather than canonical continuum; མན་ངག is pith instruction. The exact scripture/continuity sense must be reviewed occurrence by occurrence. Short ལྟ / སྒོམ at S664 keep view / cultivation; the source order ends with activity. S666 is a compressed nominal relation, tentatively “as for its meaning, there is no accomplishment of a result”; S667 is deliberately “through phenomena ... becomes obscured” for ཆོས་ཀྱིས ... བསྒྲིབས, without silently changing phenomena to a positive liberating agent. ཆོས is a provisional standalone phenomenon/phenomena use, distinct from the established whole expressions dharma embodiment and nature of phenomena.

    **Phase D current disposition (2026-10-05; supersedes earlier terminology-status claims only):** Active row `ལུང་` → **transmission**. The full row’s conditions govern, not a component-only substitution. Active row `རྒྱུད་` → **Continuum**. The full row’s conditions govern, not a component-only substitution. P1 approves tantra in titles/textual-genre contexts; continuum remains the non-literary default. Ambiguous naming constructions stay provisional. See [the whole-work review](https://github.com/Lotus-King-Translation/Mu-Tig-Phreng-Ba/blob/review/phase-d-20261005/translations/FINAL-REVIEW.md#phase-d-review). Original source-audit evidence and approval history above are retained; unresolved constructions remain flagged.

[^T05-006]: Golden-v1 chapter 5, S670, S671, S672, S673 (printed 456). སྦྱིན / ཚུལ་ཁྲིམས / བཟོད་པ are provisionally generosity / ethical discipline / patience. Their instrumental ending in S670 connects to S671, whose exact གནོད་དང་ཕན་འབྲས་བུ་བྲལ is rendered “free from the results of harm and benefit”; this is not a claim that the practices cause harm. S672 བསྒོམས་པས is a verbal/nominal cultivation form, not a new synonym. S673 མཆོག་ཏུ་བརྟགས is tentatively examined supremely; the object is implicit. Review the scope of the instrumental phrases and this superlative.

[^T05-007]: Golden-v1 chapter 5, S674, S675, S676, S677, S678, S680, S681, S682, S683 (printed 456–457). The ritual list uses local proposals: དཀྱིལ་འཁོར mandala, ལྷ deity, ས་བོན seed, སྔགས mantra, ཕྱག་རྒྱ seal, མཆོད་པ offering, དབང empowerment, དམ་ཚིག commitment, བསྙེན་པ approach, སྒྲུབ་པ accomplishment, and གདོན obstructing spirit. Canonical four empowerments does not establish a separate bare empowerment entry. Ritual seals are not the whole-expression Mahamudra entry. S681 འཁོར་ལོ is left as wheels rather than silently equated with mandalas. These are negations within the speaker’s description. The present “commitment” for དམ་ཚིག is a local English proposal; the saved chapter 4 draft used provisional “samaya.” Neither wording is owner-approved, and this choice does not silently amend that chapter or establish a universal default. Their technical senses require glossary review.

    **Phase D current disposition (2026-10-05; supersedes earlier terminology-status claims only):** Active row `དཀྱིལ་འཁོར་` → **mandala**. The full row’s conditions govern, not a component-only substitution. Active row `ཕྱག་རྒྱ་` → **seal**. The full row’s conditions govern, not a component-only substitution. P2 permits gesture in actual bodily movement, but not mudra as a third default; Mahamudra is a different whole expression. Active row `དམ་ཚིག་` → **sacred pledge**. The full row’s conditions govern, not a component-only substitution. Active row `ཡུལ་` → **object**. The full row’s conditions govern, not a component-only substitution. PD-016, MTP-000200: `དམ་ཚིག`; current English **sacred pledges** replaces “commitments”. See [the whole-work review](https://github.com/Lotus-King-Translation/Mu-Tig-Phreng-Ba/blob/review/phase-d-20261005/translations/FINAL-REVIEW.md#phase-d-review). Original source-audit evidence and approval history above are retained; unresolved constructions remain flagged.

[^T05-008]: Golden-v1 chapter 5, S684, S685, S686, S687, S688, S689, S690, S691, S692 (printed 457). S684 དེ་ནི་ཡིན་འགྲོ་བའང་ཡིན is provisionally “It is that, and is also beings”; འགྲོ་བ can also mean going, and the compressed first clause has no explicit complement beyond དེ. The sense and reference remain for review. S685 སེམས་ལས་བྱུང་བ is represented compositionally as what arises from ordinary mind, without pre-emptively making it the later སེམས་བྱུང technical compound. S687 འཁོར / མྱང་འདས are provisionally saṃsāra / nirvāṇa. S690 bare གྲོལ་བ is liberation, a proposal distinct from established self-liberation and primordial liberation. S692 “cutting [life] off” supplies the object from immediately preceding སྲོག; the verse is an identification within the speech, not an imperative. S688 retains gradual entry, and S689 distinguishes being phenomena from appearing as phenomena.

    **Phase D current disposition (2026-10-05; supersedes earlier terminology-status claims only):** Active row `མྱང་འདས་` → **transcendence of sorrow**. The full row’s conditions govern, not a component-only substitution. PD-005, MTP-000203: `འཁོར`; current English **cyclic existence** replaces “saṃsāra”. PD-005, MTP-000203: `མྱང་འདས`; current English **transcendence of sorrow** replaces “nirvāṇa”. See [the whole-work review](https://github.com/Lotus-King-Translation/Mu-Tig-Phreng-Ba/blob/review/phase-d-20261005/translations/FINAL-REVIEW.md#phase-d-review). Original source-audit evidence and approval history above are retained; unresolved constructions remain flagged.

[^T05-009]: Golden-v1 chapter 5, S694, S695, S696, S697, S698, S699, S700, S701, S702, S703 (printed 458). The implied subject continues from the preceding identity sequence; no new named agent is supplied. The provisional technical expressions are འཇིག་རྟེན་གསུམ three worlds; དགེ virtue; ཕ་རོལ་ཕྱིན་པ perfection; ས ground; ཡོན་ཏན quality; and ཐེག་པ vehicle. The listed perfection of discerning knowing does not by itself assign the bare perfection term. S696 འབྲས is a short result form; S697 retains the explicit ten without filling in names. S702 ཆེ་ཆུང་རིམ་པ preserves greater/lesser stages, without naming doctrinal vehicles. All these local mappings remain proposed.

    **Phase D current disposition (2026-10-05; supersedes earlier terminology-status claims only):** Active row `འབྲས་བུ་` → **Result**. The full row’s conditions govern, not a component-only substitution. P1 permits botanical fruit in the actual tree/flower similes; result is not forced there. See [the whole-work review](https://github.com/Lotus-King-Translation/Mu-Tig-Phreng-Ba/blob/review/phase-d-20261005/translations/FINAL-REVIEW.md#phase-d-review). Original source-audit evidence and approval history above are retained; unresolved constructions remain flagged.

[^T05-010]: Golden-v1 chapter 5, S704, S705, S706, S707, S708, S710, S711, S712, S713 (printed 458–459). S704 links “however they appear” to the following elemental list; S706 འབྱུང་བ་ཆེ is provisionally great elements, without silently completing the Tibetan to a conventional long phrase. The source lists earth, water, fire, wind, space in that order. S707–S710 continues across the page marker: ཆེན་པོ་དེ is that great [one], with its noun left implicit. ཡང་དག is provisionally authentically. Gods, demigods, humans, hell beings, animals, and hungry ghosts are provisional class terms, not an imported scheme beyond the six named items. S711–S713 lists desire, hatred, bewilderment, pride and jealousy, then predicates magical display of arising; no missing copula is asserted. These affective terms are provisional and remain distinct from canonical ignorance and delusion.

    **Phase D current disposition (2026-10-05; supersedes earlier terminology-status claims only):** Family review `གཏི་མུག`: Local provisional realization; the five-affliction lists at 000212 and 000438 and the noose at 000367 support one sense. The print-only list associated with 000021 remains in its annotation layer. Bewilderment is retained for distinct རྨོངས་པ, and ignorance/delusion/dullness are not merged. Shared assignment still Proposed. PD-024, MTP-000212: `གཏི་མུག`; current English **deluded dullness** replaces “bewilderment”. This is a linked local provisional realization under §8.1, not an approved shared default. See [the whole-work review](https://github.com/Lotus-King-Translation/Mu-Tig-Phreng-Ba/blob/review/phase-d-20261005/translations/FINAL-REVIEW.md#phase-d-review). Original source-audit evidence and approval history above are retained; unresolved constructions remain flagged.

[^T05-011]: Golden-v1 chapter 5, S714, S715, S716, S717 (printed 459). The list preserves canonical ordinary mind and mental faculty; S714 མ་རིག is the short ignorance form. S716 རྣམ་པར་ཤེས་པ is an expanded form provisionally assigned consciousness in this technical list, rather than an indiscriminate replacement for knowing. ཕུང་པོ / ཁམས / སྐྱེ་མཆེད / དབང་པོ are provisionally aggregates / elements / sense bases / faculties. The whole-expression sense gates and graded faculty entries do not independently establish every bare faculty occurrence. The explicit five is retained without an added enumeration.

[^T05-012]: Golden-v1 chapter 5, S718, S719, S720 (printed 459). Here སྒྲ occurs alongside smell, taste and touch. “Sound” is an explicit, local contextual proposal against the canonical “word,” not a new default. རེག་བྱ is provisionally objects of touch and འདོད་ཡོན objects of desire; the latter may denote desirable sensory objects, but the source does not provide an additional list. Canonical adornment remains intact. Review this sensory-context exception before glossary activation.

    **Phase D current disposition (2026-10-05; supersedes earlier terminology-status claims only):** Active row `སྒྲ་` → **Word**. The full row’s conditions govern, not a component-only substitution. P1 approves sound in auditory/sensory uses and word in linguistic uses; this does not settle the separate phrase construction. See [the whole-work review](https://github.com/Lotus-King-Translation/Mu-Tig-Phreng-Ba/blob/review/phase-d-20261005/translations/FINAL-REVIEW.md#phase-d-review). Original source-audit evidence and approval history above are retained; unresolved constructions remain flagged.

[^T05-013]: Golden-v1 chapter 5, S721, S722, S723 (printed 459). S721 exact གསོག་དང་ཡ་མ་བརླ is provisionally hollowness and insubstantiality. The unusual source form ཡ་མ་བརླ is preserved in the note rather than silently normalized; the golden source decision reports print support for its letters. Its exact idiomatic force remains uncertain. S722 སྙིང་པོ་མེད is lack of a core, keeping core a provisional standalone term, distinct from canonical essence ངོ་བོ. S723 retains both expressiveness and play. Review the two initial nouns and do not merge untranscribed commentary into the root.

[^T05-049]: Golden-v1 chapter 5, S1049, S1051 (printed 479–480). The colophon is kept separate from the root speech, and its two parts remain divided by the source page marker. མུ་ཏིག་ཕྲེང་བ is provisionally String of Pearls; གསང་བའི་རྒྱུད is secret tantra, a title-specific proposed sense of canonical continuum’s headword. The placement of precious follows the Tibetan without settling whether it qualifies the work-title or the tantra-description more narrowly. S1051 གནས་ལུགས་འཁྲུལ་པ་རང་རྫོགས་སུ་བསྟན is tentatively teaches the natural state and delusion as self-complete; taking delusion with natural state in a tighter relation is also possible. Canonical natural state and delusion remain intact; bare self-completeness is still provisional. The explicit chapter number five is retained. Review title attachment before a final human edition.

    **Phase D current disposition (2026-10-05; supersedes earlier terminology-status claims only):** Active row `རྒྱུད་` → **Continuum**. Ambiguous naming constructions stay provisional. Evidence: [Adzom p. 479, image 489](https://github.com/Lotus-King-Translation/Mu-Tig-Phreng-Ba/blob/main/evidence/golden/05/00489.png); 489row6 right title formula; 490row1 left chapter title through ལྔ་པའོ, ending before the next དེ་ནས; [Adzom p. 480, image 490](https://github.com/Lotus-King-Translation/Mu-Tig-Phreng-Ba/blob/main/evidence/golden/05/00490.png); 489row6 right title formula; 490row1 left chapter title through ལྔ་པའོ, ending before the next དེ་ནས.

Exact སྤྱོད་ཡུལ at S662 is provisionally “domain,” while bare ཡུལ at S683 is also locally “domain” in the phrase about obstructing spirits and harm. The first is a whole domain-of-activity construction, not a change to standalone canonical activity; the second differs in context from the proposed object uses elsewhere. The shared English is disclosed rather than treated as proof of Tibetan identity. Review both limited senses independently.
